(i-ḷ) uriyāvatu, maṟutalikkaṭāa māṟṟamu muṭittāka, aiyappaṭṭu niṟṟalu maruṇṭu niṟṟalu nīkki, taṉṉūlāṉātal, apporuṇ muṭivuṟakkūṟiṉa nūlāṉātal teḷiyavoru poruḷi yoṟṟumippaṭuttu, ituvē poruḷeṉat tuṇital uriyiṟ kiyalpeṉṟavāṟu.
māṟṟamumuṭittāki yeṉṟavummiyāl viṭiyumuṭit tāki yeṉka.
pērāciriyam :
ituvuṅ kāṇṭikipōla uriyum iruvakitteṉpatu aṟiyumāṟṟāṉ eytiyataṉmēṟ ciṟappuviti yuṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i - ḷ) maṟutalik kaṭāa māṟṟamum uṭittāy---maṟutali māṟṟattiṉi iṭiceṟittuk kaṭāvutalum ataṟku maṟumāṟṟamākiya viṭi kūṟutalum uṭittāy: taṉ nūlāṉum --- uriyeḻutuvāṉāṟ kūṟappaṭukiṉṟa uri taṉakku mutaṉūlākiya cūttirattāṉum, itaṉāṉē uriyiṉṟic cūttiramē nūleṉappaṭuvatūu māyiṟṟu; muṭinta nūlāṉum ataṉ mutaṉūlāṉum; muṭintanūleṉpatu iṇinūleṉpārum uḷar: iṇivaṉa kūṟat tāṉ kūṟāṉāyiṉ atu kuṉṟak kūṟaleṉa maṟukka. aṅṅaṉaṅ koḷḷum poruṇmiyuḷavāyiṉ avi,
cūttirat tuṭporu ḷaṉṟiyum
eṉpataṉāṉ aṭaṅkum.
maṟṟu, mutaṉūlāṟ kūṟiya poruḷ cila kuṉṟakkūṟiṉāṉ pōla muṭinta nūlāṟ koḷka. eṉṟateṉṉiyeṉiṉ aṟṟaṉṟu; ipporuṇmi muṭinta nūliṉum uṇṭeṉṟu eṭuttuk kāṭṭappaṭumeṉṟā ṉeṉpatu; itu, mēl;
ētu naṭiyiṉu meṭuttuk kāṭṭiṉum	(tol-mara: 102)
eṉṟavaḻip peṟutumāyiṉum mutaṉūlallatu eṭuttuk kāṭṭap peṟāareṉṟaṟku īṇṭu varintu kūṟiṉāṉeṉpatu; eṉavē, iṇinūlum avaṟṟu vaḻinūlum eṭuttuk kāṭṭuṅkāl taṉakku mutalvarāyiṉāri nāṭṭi avar karuttē paṟṟip piṟar ceytāreṉiṉallatu piṟarmēl taliminiṟīi avar karuttuppaṟṟi ivar ceytāreṉak kūṟār. aṅṅaṉaṅ kūṟiṉ avar nūṟkē uriyeḻutuvā ṉallaṉōveṉa maṟukka.
iṉi,
viṉiyi ṉīṅki viḷaṅkiya vaṟiviṉ muṉiva
	(tol-mara : 94)
ṉāṟ ceyyappaṭṭa mutaṉūṟkāyiṉ muṭintanūl eṭuttuk kāṭṭuta leṉṉum ivvilakkaṇamiṉṟeṉpatu koḷka; oru taliyaṉmiyeṉṉum uttivakipaṟṟi yeṉpatu.
aiyamum maruṭkiyuñ cevvitiṉ nīṅki---aiyavuṇarvum poyyuṇarvuñ cemporuḷiṉāṉ nīkki; teṟṟeṉa oru poruḷ oṟṟumi koḷīi---accemporuḷ karuviyākak kēṭpāṉ uṇarvu maruṭki nīkki meyyuṇarntu teṟṟeṉavum  iraṭṭuṟutaṉīkki oṟṟumi koḷuttiyum; tuṇivoṭu niṟṟal eṉmaṉār pulavar---kavarpaṭac collātu oruporuḷ tuṇinturittu māṟutalum atikārattāṉ niṉṟa uriyilak kaṇam (e-ṟu).
maṟutaliyum māṟṟamumeṉpatu kaṭā viṭi; aiyamum maruṭkiyuñ cevvitiṉīkkal orupuṭi yoppumiyuṭiya pōliyum, ataṟku oṉṟum iyipillāta poypporuḷu meṉappaṭum.
piṟitu piṟitēṟṟalu muruputoka varutalum
	(tol-col-vēṟ. ma : 21)
eṉṟavaḻip piṟiteṉṟu orukāṟ colla āṟāmuru peṉavum, piṉṉorukāṟ piṟiteṉa oḻinta urupellān taḻuvu meṉavuñ collutal pōliyeṉappaṭum; eṉṉi? avvāṟāvataṉōṭu ēḻāmurupum piṟvurupēṟkum vaḻakkuḷvaḻi oṉṟaṉik koṇṭamiyiṉ aḵtu ōrupuṭi yoppumi yuṭittāki apporuḷaṉṟeṉavumpaṭātu maṟṟoṉṟiṉik kōṭal poruḷeṉat tuṇiyavumpaṭātu aiyavuṇarvu ceytamiyiṉ, atu pōliyāyiṟṟu.
iṉip
piṟitupiṟi tēṟṟalum uruputoka varutalum
eṉṉum iraṇṭummiyum poruḷilaveṉṟu piṟitu piṟi tēṟṟalum uruputoka varuvataṟkaṇṇēṉṟu poruḷ kūṟutal pōlvaṉa, cūttirattiṉ karuttaṟiyātu poyyi meyyeṉṟu mayaṅkiya maruṭki yeṉappaṭum. ivviraṇṭaṉiyum nīkki uṇmi uṇarttutal uriyeṉappaṭuvatāyiṟṟu. niṟṟaleṉpatu niṟka avvuri yeṉṟavāṟu. taṉ nūlāṉum mutaṉūlāṉum aiyamum maruṭkiyum nīṅkuṅkāl avaṟṟuṭ kiṭanta cem poruḷāṉē nīkkappaṭumeṉpatu iraṇṭu kaṇṇāṉuṅ kūrmiyāṟ pārttā ṉeṉpatu pōlak koḷka.
āyvuri :
iḵtu, eytiyataṉ mēṟciṟappu viti kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) cūttirap poruḷi iṭimaṟittu viṉavum viṉāvum avviṉāviṟku maṟumoḻiyākiya viṭiyum uṭiyatāyt tāṉ uri ceytaṟkuriya nūliṉ cūttirattālum annūliṉ poruṇmi muṟṟa muṭinta nūlākiya mutaṉūlālum aiyavuṇarviyum tiripuṇarviyum aṟavē nīkkik kavarpaṭu poruḷiṉṟit teḷivāka oruporuḷi oṟṟumippaṭuttu itaṟku ituvē poruḷ eṉattuṇinturikka eṉa aṟi vuṟuttuvar pulavar e---ṟu.
maṟutalik kaṭāa .. iṭimaṟittut taṭi ceyvatākiya viṉā. māṟṟam --- avviṉāviṟku maṟumoḻiyākiya viṭi. taṉṉūl eṉṟatu, uriyāciriyaṉ tāṉ uriyeḻutat tērntu eṭuttuk koṇṭa nūliṉi, muṭinta nūl eṉṟatu, taṉatu nūliṉ poruṇmiyiṉi muṟṟa muṭittuk kūṟuvatākiya mutal nūliṉi. aiyamāvatu, itaṟkup poruḷ ituvō atuvō eṉa oṉṟiṉun tuṇivupiṟavāta niliyiluḷḷa pala taliyāya uṇarvu. maruṭki---mayakkam; eṉṟatu oṉṟi maṟṟoṉṟākak koḷḷum tiripuṇarviṉi. cevvitiṉ nīkkalāvatu, itaṟku ituvē poruḷ eṉayāvarum nēritiṉ ēṟṟuk koḷḷumpaṭi aiyattiṉiyum mayakkattiṉiyum aṟavē kaḷital, teṟṟeṉa ---teḷivāka, oruporuḷ oṟṟumi koḷuvutalāvatu, ituvum poruntum atuvum poruntum eṉak kavarpaṭac collāmal, itaṟku ituvē poruḷ eṉapporuḷoṟṟumi kāṭṭit taṇinturittal.	(106)
107.	collap paṭṭaṉa ellā māṇpum
maṟutali yāyiṉ maṟṟatu citivē.
iḷampūraṇam :
(i-ḷ) mēlavaṟṟiṟ kōtalāṉa nūṟkuriyatōr marapu mutaṉūlāyiṟ citivilli yeṉṟavāṟu,
eṉṉi āvaṉa kūṟiyatu viriyakalātaṉa citivatu vaḻi nūleṉṟavāṟām.
pērāciriyam :
itu, vaḻinūṟkē āvatōr vēṟupāṭuṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i ḷ) mēṟkūṟiya cūttiramuṅ, kāṇṭikiyum, uriyu meṉṉum mūṉṟaṟkuñ collappaṭṭa ilakkaṇamellāñ citiyātu māṭcimippaṭiṉum, mutaṉūloṭu māṟu koḷḷiṉ avaṟṟāṉ ellāñ citintateṉavē paṭum annūl e-ṟu.
maṟutaliyāyiṉu meṉṟa ummi etiratutaḻīiyiṟṟu; mēṟkūṟum pattuvakik kuṟṟamumē (653) yaṉṟi vaḻinūṟku ituvuṅ kuṟṟamāmeṉṟamiyiṉ1 īriṅkuṟṟamumiṉṟi eṉpataṉi nōkka iṟantatu taḻīiyaṟṟumām. itaṉāṉē, piṟ kālattu nūlceyvār nūlilakkaṇam piṟaḻāmaṟceyyiṉum muṟkālattu nūloṭu poruṇ māṟupaṭac ceyyiṉ atu marapaṉṟu vaḻinūṟkeṉa vilakkiyavāṟāyiṟṟu. eṉavē, mutaṉūṟ kāyiṉ ivvārāycciyiṉṟeṉpatu karuttu; eṉṉi? mutalvaṉūṉ māṟupaṭuvataṟku ataṉ muṉṉiyatōr nūl iṉmiyiṉeṉpatu. allatūum maṟṟatu eṉṟu orumi kūṟiṉamiyāṉum itu vaḻinūṟkē vilakkiṟṟeṉpatu koḷka.2 maṟṟu mēl,
mutalum vaḻiyumeṉa nutaliya neṟiyiṉa
	(tol. mara : 39)
eṉavē, mutaṉūliṉ vaḻittu vaḻinūleṉpatūum, maṟutali yāyiṟ citiveṉpatūum peṟutumātaliṉ iccūttiram mikiyām piṟaveṉiṉ, - aṟṟaṉṟu; mutalvaḻiyeṉpaṉa muṉṉum piṉṉum kāṭṭi ṉaṉṟi māṟupaṭāmik kūṟalvēṇṭu meṉpatūum peṟutumākaliṉ atu kūṟalvēṇṭumeṉpatu peṟāmākaliṉ itu kūṟalvēṇṭumeṉpatu. allākkāl,
		muṉṉōr nūliṉmuṭipuoruṅ kovvāmip
piṉṉōṉ vēṇṭum vikaṟpaṅ kūṟuvāṉ
cellumeṉpatu.3
āyvuri :
itu vaḻinūṟkē yāvatōr vēṟupāṭu uṇarttukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) mēṟkūṟappaṭṭa ilakkaṇamellām citiyāmal māṭcimippaṭiṉum mutaṉūloṭu māṟupaṭavariṉum annūl citivuṭiyateṉavē paṭum e-ṟu.
maṟutaliyātal --- mutalnūlporuḷoṭu māṟupaṭutal citivu --- kuṟṟam. mēṟkūṟappaṭum kūṟiyatu kūṟal mutalākiya pattuk kuṟṟaṅkaḷuṭaṉ iṅṅaṉam mutaṉūloṭu māṟukoḷḷa variṉum atuvum kuṟṟamākavē koḷḷappaṭum eṉa aṟi vuṟuttuvār. maṟutaliyāyiṉum maṟṟatu citivē eṉṟār āyiṉum eṉpuḻi ummi mēṟkūṟappaṭum kūṟiyatu kūṟal mutalākiya kuṟṟappaṭa varutalēyaṉṟi ivvāṟu mutaṉūloṭu māṟupaṭavariṉ kuṟṟamām eṉa etiratu taḻīi vantamiyiṉ etiratu taḻīiya eccavummiyākum eṉak koṇṭār pērāciriyar. iṉi, maṟutaliyāyiṉ eṉṟa pāṭam iḷampūraṇar urippatippiṟ kāṇappaṭukiṟatu.	(107)
108.	citivila eṉpa mutalvaṉ kaṇṇē
pērāciriyam :
itu mēṟkūṟiya vaḻinūṟkuppōla mutaṉūṟkē āvatōr vēṟupāṭuṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ) mēṟ potuvakiyāṟ kūṟappaṭṭa nūlilakkaṇam aintaṉuḷ īriṅkuṟṟamumiṉṟiyeṉa muṉṉē ōtiya ilakkaṇam oṉṟu: mutaṉūṟkāyiṉ ammarapiṉa ilakkaṇam vēṇṭuvataṉṟu1 (e-ṟu).
citivilakkaṇattic citiveṉṟu ōtiṉāṉ āku peyarāṉeṉa uṇarka. maṟṟu,
viṉiyi ṉīṅki viḷaṅkiya aṟiviṉ muṉivaṟku	(649)
citivilaveṉpatu aṟivāṉē uṇarvaleṉiṉ, eṭuttōttuk kaḷintu uyttuṇartal payamiṉṟeṉpatu. allatūum mutaṉūṟku muṉṉiyatōr nūliṉi ilakkiyamākap peṟiṉaṉṟē mutalvaṉṟāṉ nūlilakkaṇañ ceyvatu.
maṟṟu aṉṉatōr nūliṉ avi ceyyāta nūlilakkaṇam yāṉ ceytēṉeṉavum avaṟṟuc citiviṉiyē mutaṉūṟkaṇ illiyeṉṟatūum, oḻinta nāṉkum mutalvaṉūṟkaṇṇē uḷavākaliṉ avaṟṟi ilakkaṇaṅ kūṟiṉāṉ eṉavum, iṉik kuṟṟaṅkaḷum piṟṟōṉṟuṅkoleṉṟu añci iṅṅaṉam avaṟṟi variyaṟuttu yāṉ pātukākkiṉṟēṉeṉavum, aṅṅaṉam pātukāttu ivvilakkaṇaṅkaḷ ōtiṟṟum avaṉ mutaṉūlē ilakkiyamākap peṟṟaṉavuṅ kūṟiyatu kūṟal (663) pōlvaṉa vēṟōr poruḷ viḷakkumāyiṟ kuṟṟamaṉṟeṉpa taṉiyum yāṉē kūṟiyataṉṟi mutalvaṉāyiṟ cilvakiyeḻuttiṉ ceyyuṭṭākavē avaṟṟi vēṟuvēṟu vitittup parantu paṭac ceyyumeṉavum, iviyellām aṟivittaṟkuc,
citivila veṉpa mutalvaṉ kaṇ	(661)
eṉṟāṉeṉpatu.
maṟṟu, mutalvaṉyāppē kūṟumāyiṉ annūṟkut tantiravuttiyum vēṇṭuvateṉṟām piṟaveṉiṉ aṅṅaṉamē, mutaṉūṟkāyiṉ muppattiruvakiyutti vārātu ciṟupāṉmiyāṉ varumeṉṟaṟkaṉṟē,
		īriṅ kuṟṟamu miṉṟi nēritiṉ
muppat tiruvaki yuttiyoṭu puṇarntatu	(653)
eṉa īṇṭu vilakkappaṭṭa kuṟṟattiṉ piṉṉar avvuttiyi vitta karutteṉpatu āṇṭuṅ kūṟuvāmāyiṟṟeṉpatu.
maṟṟu, mutaṉūliṉāl nūl ilakkaṇaṅ kūṟāṉōveṉiṉ, kūṟiṉāṉē yaṉṟō? tāṉ oruvakiyāṉ nūlceytu maṟṟu atuvē nūlilakkaṇameṉap piṟkālattārkku aṟiyavit tamiyiṉeṉpatu.1 maṟṟuc citivilaveṉpār yārōveṉiṉ nikaḻkālattāciriyarum etirkālattāciriyarumeṉa uṇarka.
āyvuri :
itu mutaṉūṟkuriyatōr marapuṇarttukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) : mutalvaṉ ceyta nūliṉkaṇṇē kūṟiyatu kūṟal mutaliya kuṟṟamuṭiya ilakkaṇaṅkaḷ uḷavākā eṉpar periyōr e-ṟu.
mutalnūl ceytavaṉ viṉiyiṉ nīṅki viḷaṅkiya aṟiviṉ muṉivaṉ ātalāṉum, avaṉ nūlceytaṟkumuṉ ataṟku mutaṉūlākap piṟitoru nūl iṉmiyāṉum mutalvaṉ kaṇ citivu ila eṉpa eṉṟār citivu - kuṟṟam; citivuṭiya ilakkaṇattic citivu eṉṟār.
innūṟpāvukku iḷampūraṇaruri kiṭikkavilli. (108)
109.	mutalvaḻi yāṉum yāppiṉuṭcitiyum
vallōṉ puṉiyā vāram pōṉṟē.
iḷampūraṇam :
vaḻinūṟ kuriyatōr marapuṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i--ḷ) mutaṉūliṉ vaḻicceyyiṉum annūl yāppiṉuṭ citiyum vallavaṉ puṉiyāta vārampōla veṉṟavāṟu.
pērāciriyam :
itu, mutaṉūloṭu maṟutalippaṭutal vaḻinūṟkuk kuṟṟa meṉkiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) mutalvaḻiyāyiṉum yāppiṉuṭcitiyum --- atikārattāṉē mutaṉūliṉik kuṟṟamiṉṟi varuvateṉṟu īṇṭu ilakkaṇaṅkūṟal vēṇṭuvataṉṟeṉṟāṉ iṉi ammutaṉūliṉi māṟupaṭāmal āṇṭōtiya poruṇmi kūṟumāyiṉum, nālvaki yāppiṉuṇ mutaṉiṉṟa mūṉṟumpaṟṟi vaḻi nūṟkuk kuṟṟam piṟakkum; maṟṟu moḻipeyarttaloḻiyak koḷḷumāṟeṉṉi yeṉiṉ, yāppiṉuṭ citiyu meṉṟu iṭamum iṭattiyal poruḷumāka ōtiṉākaliṉ aḵtoḻikkap paṭum. maṟṟu aḵtoḻikkumāṟeṉṉi yeṉiṉ, --- tamiḻ nāṭṭu vaḻakkiṟku mutaṉūlākiya akattiyattuṇ moḻi peyarttuc ceyyavēṇṭum poruḷilavākalāṉum, piṟa pāṭikkum potuvāyiṉa poruḷavāyiṉ aviyun tamiḻ vaḻakku nōkkiyē ilakkaṇañ ceyyappaṭumākalāṉum, avarkkum moḻipeyarttal vēṇṭuvataṉṟākaliṉ atuvē oḻikkappaṭum. ikkaruttiṉāṉaṉṟē,
vaḻiyi ṉeṟiyē nālvakit tākum
	(tol. mara : 96)
eṉa vaḻinūliyē virittu nālvaki yāppiṟkum urimi kūṟiyateṉpatu.1 aṅṅaṉam mutaṉūṟku moḻipeyarttaliṉ miyiṉ yāppoṉṟumpaṟṟic citivupiṟavātu, oḻintaṉa paṟṟi vaḻinūṟkuc citivuṇṭāmeṉpatu karuttu.
vallōṉ puṇarā vāram pōṉṟē - vāram puṇarppāṉ vēṟoruvaṉāyavaḻi mutaṟkūṟu puṇarntāṟ puṇarntavāṟṟōṭu poruntac ceyyātavāṟupōla (e-ṟu).
vārameṉpatu kūṟu; eṉṉi? oru pāṭṭiṉip piṟ kūṟucolluvāri.
vāram pāṭun tōriya makaḷirum
	(cilap 14: 155)
eṉpavākaliṉ,
yāppiṉuṭ cititaleṉpatu2 mutaṉūlum vaḻakku nūlāyiṉ iḻintōr vaḻakkum vaḻakkaṉṟōveṉak kūṭṭi virittu yāttucceyyiṉum. aḻāṉ puḻāṉ (tol. eḻuttu : 316) eṉpaṉa pōlvaṉa ikkālattilaveṉṟu kaḷintu tokuttu yāttucceyyiṉun, tokiviriyum mayaṅkumāṟṟāṉ virintatu tokuttaleṉṉum nūṟpuṇarppiṉit tokiviriyeṉum yāppeṉak kūṟiyatallātavaḻittokuttu yāttē ceytalum, immūvaki yāppiṉoṭu meyttiṟaṅ kūṟuvaleṉa moḻi peyarttali mayakkik kūṟutalumellām yāppiṉuṭ citivēyām. atu paṇṇum pāṇiyum mutalāyiṉa oppiṉum vallōṉ puṇartta iṉṉiciyataṉ nīrmippaṭak kāṭṭā; vāram puṇarppāṉ puṟanīratākiya icipaṭap puṇarttal pōlvatāyiṟṟu.
maṟṟuiḻintōr vaḻakkiṉip piṟkālattu uyarntōrun takutipaṟṟi vaḻaṅkupavākalāṉ aviyum amiyāvōveṉiṉ avi cāṉṟōr ceyyuṭkutavātu; kaṟṟuṇarntārum kaṟṟuṇarātārum maṟṟu avi kēṭṭē maṉamakiḻvārippaṟṟi nikaḻntaṉavāyiṉum, oṟṟumippaṭṭu oruvaki nillā, peṟṟa kālantōṟum piṟitupiṟitākik kaṭṭaḷippaṭuttu nūl cey vārkkuṅ kiyikantu, variyaṟi yiṉṟāmākaliṉ aḵtu ilakkaṇameṉappaṭāteṉpatu.1
āyvuri :
itu, vaḻinūṟku āvatōr marapuṇarttukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) mutaṉūliyē aṭiyoṟṟi māṟupaṭāmi aṅku ōtiya poruḷiyē vaḻinūl kūṟumāyiṉum, icivallavaṉāṟ puṇarkkappaṭāta vāra ici pōṉṟu, (tokuttal, virittal, tokiviri, moḻipeyarppu eṉṉum) yāppuvaki kāraṇamāka vaḻinūlil ilakkaṇac citivu ēṟpaṭutal uṇṭu. (attakiya citivu nērātavāṟu vaḻinūl ceyka) e-ṟu.
yāppu eṉṟatu, vaḻi nūliṉ amippu muṟiyākiya tokuttal mutaliya nālvakiyāppiṉi. mutaṉūlāciriyaṉ kūṟiya poruṇmiyiṉi aṭiyoṟṟi vaḻinūl ceyyuṅkāl tokutturikkattakkaṉa ivi, viritturikkat takkaṉa ivi, ivvirutiṟamum amiyak kūṟattakkaṉa iviyeṉṟu ārāyn tuṇarntu, avaṟṟi ammuṟiyil yāttamittal kaṟṟut tuṟipōyiṉārk kallatu ēṉiyōrkku attuṇi eḷitaṉ ṟākaliṉ mutalvaḻiyāyiṉum yāppiṉuṭ citiyum eṉṟār. vallōṉ eṉṟatu. icit tuṟiyil vallavaṉi. vāram eṉpatu, icippāṭalil muṟkūṟu pāṭuvārkkuriya iciyuṭaṉ poruntap piṟkūṟupāṭuvōrkkuriyatāka amikkappeṟum iciyamippiṉi, icittuṟiyil vallavaṉ allātavaṉāl amikkappaṭum vāra ici muṟkūṟṟiṉuṭaṉ iṇitaliṉṟi vaḻuppaṭutal pōṉṟu, nūṟpuṇarp paṟiyātāṉ ceyta vaḻi nūl yāppu vakiyāl mutaṉūloṭu māṟukoṇṭu vaḻuppaṭum eṉpatām. mutaṉūliṉ coṟporuḷamippiṉi aṭiyoṟṟi ataṉ vaḻiyil poruḷpiṟaḻāmi moḻi peyarttal vēṇṭum eṉpataṉi moḻi peyarttu atarppaṭa yāttal eṉa muṉṉark kūṟiṉārātaliṉ moḻipeyarppu yāppu oṉṟu nīṅkalāka ēṉiya tokuttal mutaliya mūṉṟuyāppiṉi iccūttiram cuṭṭiyatākak koḷvar pērāciriyar.	(109)
110.	citiveṉap paṭupavi vaciyaṟa nāṭiṟ
kūṟiyatu kūṟal māṟukoḷak kūṟal
kuṉṟak kūṟal mikipaṭak kūṟal
poruḷila kūṟal mayaṅkak kūṟal
kēṭpōrk kiṉṉā yāppiṟ ṟātal
paḻitta moḻiyāṉ iḻukkak kūṟal
taṉṉāṉ oruporuḷ karutik kūṟal
eṉṉa vakiyiṉum maṉaṅkōḷ iṉmi
aṉṉa piṟavum avaṟṟuviri yākum.
iḷampūraṇam :
mēlatikārappaṭṭa īriṅ kuṟṟamum āmāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ) kūṟiyatu kūṟalāvatu orukāṟ kūṟiyataṉip piṉṉuṅkūṟal.
māṟukoḷak kūṟalāvatu orukāṟ kūṟiya poruḷōṭu māṟu koḷḷumāṟu piṉkūṟal aḵtāvatu tavamnaṉṟu eṉṟavaṉṟāṉ tavan tīteṉṟu kūṟal.
kuṉṟak kūṟalāvatu tāṉatikaritta poruḷkaḷuṭ cilavaṟṟik kūṟātoḻital.
mikipaṭak kūṟalāvatu atikārap poruḷaṉṟip piṟa poruḷuṅ kūṟutal. aḵtāvatu tamiḻilakkaṇañ colluvā ṉeṭuttuk koṇṭāṉ vaṭamoḻiyilakkaṇamum kūṟal.
poruḷila kūṟalāvatu muṉṉum piṉṉum varukiṉṟa poruṇmik koppiṉṟip payaṉillātaṉa kūṟal.
mayaṅkak kūṟalāvatu kēṭṭārkkup poruḷ viḷaṅku māṟiṉṟik kūṟal.
kēṭpōrkkiṉṉā yāppiṟṟātaleṉpatu poruḷ yākkappaṭṭa cūttirañ cantaviṉpamiṉṟi yiruttal.
paḻittamoḻiyāṉ iḻukkak kūṟalāvatu tāṉoru poruḷi yoruvāypāṭṭāṟ ṟirittup piṟitoru vāy pāṭṭāṟ kūṟutal. akkuṟippu ulakavaḻak kiṉmiyāṟ piṟarkkup pulappaṭātām; ataṉāl atuvuṅ kuṟṟamāyiṟṟu.
eṉṉavakiyiṉum maṉaṅkōḷ iṉmiyāvatu eḻutti ṉāluñ colliṉāṉum poruḷiṉāṉum maṉaṅkoḷḷumāṟu kūṟāmi.
pērāciriyam :
itu, niṟutta muṟiyāṉē īriṅ kuṟṟamum uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i--ḷ) citivu eṉappaṭupavi vaciyaṟa nāṭiṉ kuṟṟa meṉṟaṟkuc ciṟappuṭiyaṉavaṟṟik kuṟṟantīra ārāyiṉ ivaṟṟi eṉappaṭupaveṉpateṉ? kuṟṟamātaṟkuc ciṟantilavām piṟaveṉiṉ,---ivvāciriyar eññāṉṟuṅ kuṟṟattiṟku ilakkaṇaṅ kūṟārākalāṉ ilakkaṇattōṭu kūṟuntuṇip payampaṭutalum oruvāṟṟāṟ ciṟappeṉavēpaṭumeṉpatu; allatūum oḻinta ceyyuṭkāyiṉ iviyaṉittum ākāveṉpatu īṇṭē taḻāal vēṇṭumākalāṉuñ ceyyuṭ kāyiṉ avi amiyāc ciṟappu uṭimiyāṉum itu vaḻakkuñ ceyyuḷumēyaṉṟi avaṟṟiṉ vēṟupaṭavuñ ceyyappaṭum nūlilakkaṇamākalāṉum eḻuvaki vaḻuppōla amivaṉavē kūṟātu nūluḷ varappeṟātaṉavum varintu kūṟiṉāṉeṉpatu amivaṉavaṟ ṟiṉañcārtti oḻintaṉavuṅ kūṟiṉāṉ; allākkāl, aviyē kuṟṟamāki oḻintaṉa pukutap peṟuvāṉ cellu meṉṟañciyeṉpatu.
vaciyaṟa nāṭiṉ eṉṟataṉāṉ iṅṅaṉaṅ kuṟṟameṉṟu variyappaṭṭaṉavaṟṟikkoṇṭu pukuntu maṟṟoru poruḷ koḷḷiṉ avi vaciyaṟṟaṉavāmeṉpatu. avi 1kūṟiyatu kūṟaṉ, 2māṟukoḷakkūṟal, 3mikipaṭakkūṟal, 4poruḷiṉ moḻitaṉ 5mayaṅkakkūṟal eṉṉum aintumāyiṉa.
1kūṟiyatukūṟal muṉṉorukāṟ colliya poruḷ piṉṉumorukāṟ collutal; atu,
uṭkuvarat tōṉṟu mīrēḻ tuṟittu	(tol-puṟat: 1)
eṉavum,
vantavīrēḻ vakiyiṟ ṟākum	(tol-puṟat: 3)
eṉavum irukāṟcolli orukāṟ payaṉkoṇṭavāṟu.
2māṟukoḷakkūṟal
marappeyark kiḷavik kammē cāriyi
	(tol-eḻut-kuṟ: 10)
eṉṟavaḻip pulliṉiyum marameṉa aṭakkutal. ik karuttu nōkki (yum) (yē) pōlum ; āṇṭu
oṉṟeṉa muṭittal taṉṉiṉa muṭittal
eṉpataṉāṟ pulliṉiyum marameṉṟu taḻīiyiṉa akkarut teṉpatu
3kuṉṟakkūṟal collappukunta poruḷiṉi ācaṟak kūṟātu oḻiyappōtal ; iḵtōr payaṉpaṭākkuṟṟam.
4mikipaṭakkūṟal cilvaki yeḻuttiṉ ceyyuṭṭākac ceyyātu cūttirattuṭ cilacoṉ mikiyākac ceytal pōlvaṉa ; avi,
āyī riyala puṇarnilic cuṭṭu	(tol-eḻut-puṇa: 5)
eṉṟāṟpōlvaṉa. iṅṅaṉam mikipaṭac ceyyuṅkāṉ muṉṉiṉṟa colliṟku oṉṟum iyipillaṉa kūṟalākā; eṉṉi?
muppāṟ puḷḷiyu meḻuttō raṉṉa.
	(tol-eḻut-nūṉ 2)
eṉṟavaḻi, avitām muppāṟ puḷḷiyu meṉap payaṉili koṇṭu māṟip piṉṉar eḻuttōraṉṉa eṉṟatu āṇṭu iyipillatōr colleṉṟu ilēcupaṭātu tāṉ collukiṉṟa poruṭkum iyipupaṭac colliyē mikip paṭuttal vēṇṭumeṉpatu : eṉṉi?
eḻutteṉappaṭupa	(tol-eḻut-1)
eṉpataṉuṇ mūṉṟumē ciṟappuṭiyaveṉpatu koṇṭāṉāyiṟ piṉṉar ammūṉṟiṉiyuñ ciṟappillā muppatiṉōṭum okkumeṉa uvamikkalākāteṉavum, māṭṭēṟṟuc cūttiraṅkaḷā ṉellām oppumi peṟuvatallatu ciṟappiṉmi kāṭṭap paṭātākalāṉum muppaḵtumē ciṟantaṉaveṉṉuṅ karut tiṉāṉāyiṉ mūṉṟuñ ciṟappilaveṉpatu mutaṟcūttirattuṭ peṟappaṭumākaliṉ, piṉṉar ivvilēcu kūṟic ciṟappaḻittal vēṇṭā vākalāṉum palaporuṭkēṟpiṉallatu kōṭala ṉṟi aiyuṟaṟṟōṟuñ cūttirañ ceyyāṉākalāṉum,
eḻuttō raṉṉa	(tol-eḻuttu : 2)
veṉpatu āṇṭu iyipillatōr colleṉṟataṉi āṇṭup peytu ilēcu koṇṭāṉeṉal ākāteṉpatu.
5poruḷilamoḻital - nūṉutaliya poruḷaṉṟi,
eṉmaṉār pulavar
eṉṟāṟ pōla vaḻinūlvāypāṭṭu māttiriyē payaṉākac ceyvaṉa.
6mayaṅkakkūṟal - niṟuttamuṟiyāṉaṉṟi.
atti ṉakara makaramuṉi yilli
	(tol-eḻut-puṇa : 23)
eṉa mayaṅkakkūṟip piṟitoṉṟukōṭal :
7kēṭpōrkkiṉṉā yāppiṟṟātal-cūttiracceyyuḷ kēṭpōrkku iṉṉāticippac ceytal.
8paḻittamoḻiyāṉ iḻukkaṅkūṟal --- muṭivallāta collāṉum iḻinta collāṉum eṭuttu muṭippaṉavaṟṟi maṟu muṭipupaṟṟi iḻukkaṅ kūṟalāyiṟṟu.
9taṉṉāṉoruporuḷ karutikkūṟal -- muṉṉōrāṟ kūṟavum paṭātu vaḻakkiṉuḷḷatumaṉṟit taṉṉuḷḷē oru poruḷ paṭittuk kūṟutal; (taṉ) āṉeṉpatu oru colleṉat tāṉeṉpatōr coṟṟāy meyyiṉuḷ uṇara niṉṟatu. taṉṉāṟ ṟāṉoruporuḷpaṭittukkuṟukac ceytaṉ mūṉṟāvateṉiṉum iḻukkātu. eṉṉi? nutalikkūṟaleṉṉum payaṉilikkut tāṉeṉṉum peyar veḷippaṭā niṉṟatu veḷippaṭuttukkoḷap peṟutumākaliṉ.
10eṉṉavakiyiṉu maṉaṅkōḷiṉmi---evvāṟṟāṉum poruḷaṟitaṟku aritākac ceytal ivināṉkuṅkuṉṟak kūṟaloṭu kūṭṭa aintum eññāṉṟum payaṉpaṭātaṉavāyiṉa.
aṉṉa piṟavum avaṟṟuviri yākum - avipōlvaṉa piṟavum īriṅkuṟṟameṉappaṭṭa toki eṇṇiṟku avvāṟu tokuttaṟkēṟṟa viriyeṇṇām, iviyum ivipōlvaṉa piṟavum (e-ṟu).
virintatu tokutta leṉpataṉāṉ etiratunōkki āṇṭut tokuttāṉātaliṉ īṇṭu avaṟṟit toki kūṟātu viritteṇṇiṉā ṉeṉpatu.
piṟavum eṉṟataṉāṉ veṟṟeṉat toṭuttaṉ maṟṟoṉṟu virittal ceṉṟu tēyntiṟutal, niṉṟu payaṉiṉmi eṉṟāṟpōlvaṉa koḷka. ivi mēl īriṅ kuṟṟamumeṉa ummiyāl taḻuviyavaṟṟu viriyāyaṉa. mutaṉūloṭu māṟutalum yāppiṉuṭ cititalum ivipōla orō vaḻi vārā annūliṉ muḻuvatūuṅ koḷḷakkiṭantamiyiṉ īṇṭavi puṟaṉaṭiyāl taḻuvappaṭṭavāṟeṉpatu. ivi yellāṅ kuṟṟameṉṟu kaḷiyappaṭuvaṉavāyiṉum īriṅ kuṟṟamumiṉṟiyeṉa mutaṟcūttirattuḷ ōtippukuntāṉ. allākkāl ivaṟṟuṭcila nūluṭ pukutumāṟum avaṟṟāṟ payaṉkoḷḷumāṟum. iṉmiyiṉeṉpatu. payaṉkoḷḷap paṭuvaṉa ainteṉpatu mēṟkāṭṭiṉām.
iṉi, appatiṉāṉkuṅ kuṟṟamēyāki varumāṟu:
		taṉmi yuvami yuruvakan tīvakam
piṉvaru niliyē muṉṉa vilakkē
vēṟṟup poruḷvippu vēṟṟumi yeṉāa
eṉavum,
		uruvaka muvami vaḻinili maṭakkē
viricuṭar viḷakkeṉa marīi varuvaṉa
eṉavuñ cila cūttiraṅkaḷi mutaṉiṟīip piṉṉarum avvāy pāṭṭāṉē,
		taṉmi yuvami yuruvakan tīvakam
piṉvaru niliyē muṉṉa vilakkē
vēṟṟup poruḷvippu vēṟṟumi yeṉṟāṅ
keṇvakiyiyava ceyyuṭ kaṇiyeṉa
miyaṟu pulavar vakutturit taṉarē
eṉṟāṟpōlap piṉṉarum avvāṟē cūttirañceytal 1kūṟiyatu kūṟalāyp payaṉpaṭātāyiṟṟu. eṉṉi? ivaṟṟatu vēṟupāṭu tokic cūttirattut tuṇinteṇṇiyataṉāṉē peṟṟavaḻip piṉṉum avvāṟē maṟṟōr cūttirañ ceytataṉāṟ payantatiṉmiyiṉeṉpatu.
		aṉṟeṉa vorutali tuṇinta kuṟṟa
naṉṟaṟi pulavar nāṭṭutaṟkuriya
eṉṟāṟpōlvaṉa variyātu kuṟṟameṉṟataṉiyē cāṉṟōrk kāyiṟ kuṟṟamākāteṉṟal 2māṟukoḷak kūṟalām, ceyyuṭ kellām aṇiyilakkaṇaṅ kūṟuvāṉ kuṟṟaṅ kūṟum vaḻi varintu kūṟutaliṉ.
ceyyuṭkuriya poruṭpaṭi yellāvaṟṟuḷḷum nallaṉavun tīyaṉavum iviyeṉṟu collappukuntāṉ avaṟṟuṭcila colliyoḻitalum, vaḻakkoṭu meyp poruḷum ārāyva leṉṟu pukuntāṉ avaṟṟuḷ vaḻakkiṟku vēṇṭuva kūṟi meypporuḷārāycci muḻuvatūuñ collātunekiḻntu pōtalum, eṭuttukkoṇṭa nūluṭ kāṭṭum ilakkiyaṅkaḷic cūttirattāṉ aṭivari ceyvaṉeṉṟu pukuntu cila maṟuttuc ceytu cilavaṟṟukkuc ceyyātu pōtal pōlvaṉavuṅ 3kuṉṟakkūṟalām. avi muṭinta nūliṟ kaṇṭu koḷka.
		iṭiyiṭi tiriyā tiyaṉeṟi marapi
ṉuṭiya karupporu ḷorōvaḻit tuvaṉṟavu
miyaṟki marapi ṉuripporu ṭōṉṟavum
paṉṉiru kālamum nālvaki yiṭattoṭun
toṉṉeṟi marapiṟ ṟēraṉṟiṉar ceyalē
eṉac cūttirañceytu nāṟpatteṭṭiṉum nāṟpatteṭṭuk kālamun tokka cūttirattāl.
		tuṭippaṉa tuṭittuc ceyaṟki pōla
vaḻiyiṭa ṉoḻittuk kālaṅ kūṟuval
eṉṟu pukuntu akkālattuḷḷuñ ciṟupoḻutu kūṟātu iṭa ilakkaṇamē kūṟi, iṭattiṟkuc curuṅka vēṟu cūttirañceytavāṟu pōlātu ataṟku iṉṟiyamiyātaṉavellāṅ kuṉṟāmaṟ kūṟātu, ceṉṟupaṭṭa parappiṟṟākac cūttirañceytal pōl vaṉa 4mikipaṭakkūṟalām. aḵtāvatu iṉṉiḷavēṇileṉpatu,
		taṇṇiḻa laṟalpāṟ ṟaṭikarit tuṟiteraṟu
milaṅku mulikka ṇēyppakkōṅ kaviḻntu
vaṇṭutā tūtun taṇṭaḷirk kāviṟ
parumala rutirntu murukukamaḻ parappiṉ
maṇvayiṟu kuḷirkkun taṇṇaṟuṅ kayattu
niḻaliruḷ naṭuva ṇaḻalavir tāmarit
tāḷkaṟit taruntum vāḷeyiṟ ṟiḷamīṉ
puḷḷuka veṟiya veḷḷeṉṟu piṟaḻum
paḷiṅku nekiḻntaṉṉa tuḷaṅkāt teṇṇīr
taḷirkuṭintu teviṭṭiya kuyilkuṉintu kuṭippa
maravan tāḻnta karimarac cāraṟ
ṟēṉāṟu tēṟal vēṉiṟ kaṇṇum
vārār kolleṉa nīrvār kaṇṇoṭu
pulampuṭi makaṭūuk kalaṅkaña reyta
yāṟuṅ kuḷaṉuṅ kāvumāṭi
yōruyir mintaru makaḷiruṅ kaḷippak
kāmaviḻavoṭu kaṉṉiyar nōṟpa
nilavuñ cāntum palavayiṟ payaṉpaṭa
tuṉpak kālan tuṭittaṉar peṟūu
miṉpak kāla meṉmaṉār pulavar.
eṉṟāṟpōlap paṟṟikkūṟip peruñcūttirañ ceyta vaḻiyum paruvattiṟku variyaṟiyilakkaṇam pōtutaliṉṟi viṭutalāyiṟṟu.
iṉi,
ammūṉ ṟeṉpa maṉṉic collē
	(tol-col-iṭi : 4)
eṉavum,
appā leṭṭē yummic collē
	(tol-col-iṭi: 7)
eṉavum,
āyī rintoṭu piṟavumaṉṉa
eṉavum iṉṉōraṉṉa palavum mikipaṭac ceytār iv vāciriyarākaviṟ piṟkālattu nūlceytārkkum mikipaṭac ceytal amiyumeṉpārumuḷar. aṟṟaṉṟu,
yāvayiṉ variṉun tokiyiṉ ṟiyalā
	(tol col-iṭi : 42)
eṉappaṭṭa eṇṇākaliṉ toki, kūṟiṉamiyāṉum vaḻakkātalāṉum, atumutalāka allatu cūttirac ceyyuḷuñ ceyyārākalāṉum, aṅṅaṉan tokitoṭarākkāl iraṇṭākkappaṭṭu vikāravakiyāl tokuttāṉāmeṉavum avi pala collākalāl tokukkappaṭuvaṉavalla veṉavuñ colli maṟukka allatūum. maṉṉeṉpatu ōr iṭiccol, atu tāṉaṭinta coṟporuḷaṉṟit taṉakku vēṟu poruḷiṉ miyiṟ poruḷ paṟṟi mūṉṟeṉalākātu, palaporuḷoru col eṉappaṭuva taṉṟi, ataṉāṉ ataṉinōkki mūṉ ṟeṉṟāṉallaṉ, aḵtu aṭutta colliṉi maṉṉic colleṉṟāṉ, avi mūṉṟaṉṟi ettuṇiyumpalavāyiṉum oṉṟāyiṉum mūṉṟu pakutippaṭumeṉṟu kōṭaṟkeṉpatu. oḻinta iṭiccollāyiṉum avaṟṟiṟkum iḵtokkum maṉṉic colleṉpatu vēṟṟumittokiyēyaṉṟip paṇputtokiyākalumuṭittu. maṉṉaṭutta colliṉiyum maṉṉeṉa ākupeyarāṟ kūṟiṉāṉeṉpatu.
		vaḻakku vaḻippaṭṭa collīṟu tiriyiṉum
paṭittuk koṇṭa colloṭu civaṇit
tiricol leṉṟē ceppiṉar pulavar
virivaḷip paṇittōṇ maṭanal lōyē
eṉṟāṟ pōla iyipillaṉa kūṟutal 5poruḷilamoḻi talām. ivi oḻinta ceyyuṭkāyiṉ taṭaṅkaṇṇāyeṉṟāṟ pōl varappeṟumeṉṟaṟku ōtteṉṉiyeṉiṉ, atu ceyyuḷ ceyvār vēṇṭiyavāṟu ceypavākaliṉ īṇṭuc cilvaki yeḻuttiṉ (655) ceyyuṭṭākac ceyyunūṟkē itu vilakkiṉamiyiṉ ataṉ tiṟattuk kaṭāviṉṟeṉa maṟukka.
6mayaṅkakkūṟaleṉpatu.
mayaṅkā marapi ṉeḻuttumuṟi kāṭṭuval	(pāyiram)
eṉṟu pukuntāṟpōla, iṉṉatu colluvateṉṟu pukuntāṉ meynnūlum vaḻakkunūlum uṭaṉārāytalum, āciriyamun tamiḻum pōlvaṉa uṭaṉārāytalum pōlvaṉa.
7kēṭpōrkkiṉṉāyāppāvatu,
katana pama
cañayava
uyirmuṉ piṉvalliṉam
larayantā meṉa
eṉṟāṟpōlac cūttiraccurukkamum, moḻikku mutalā meḻuttum īṟāmeḻuttum potuvakiyāṉ aṭaṅka ōtutaṟ payaṉōkki iṅṅaṉam iṉṉāvōcittākac cūttirañ ceytal pōlvaṉa.
8paḻittamoḻiyā ṉiḻukkaṅkūṟal,
		viḷāme ṉiṟuti paḻamoṭu puṇariṟ
ṟaḷāviyaṟ ṟaṉṟi yiyaṟki yākum
eṉṟāṟpōla viḷāmeṉpatōr vaḻuccollāṟ cūttirañ ceytal. oṉṟaṟkoṉṟeṉpataṉi,
oṉṟiṉuk koṉṟu
eṉac cūttirañ ceytalum atu.
9taṉṉāṉoruporuḷ karutik kūṟaleṉpatu,
malipaṭukaṭāttiṉi āṉantak kuṟṟameṉap piṟ kālattāṉoruvaṉ oru cūttiraṅ kāṭṭutalum, patamuṭip peṉpatōr ilakkaṇam paṭittuk kōṭalum (naṉṉūl) pōlvaṉa.
10eṉṉa vakiyiṉu maṉaṅkōḷiṉmi varumāṟu :
irutiṇip piṟanta vimpāṟ kiḷavikkum
	(tol-col-peyar: 7)
eṉṉuñ cūttirattiṟkum,
eppeyar muṉṉarum valleḻuttu varuvaḻi
	(tol-eḻut-puṇar: 26)
eṉpataṟkum vēṟuporuḷ urippārurikkumāṟṟāṉ aṟiyappaṭum.
11iṉi maṟṟoṉṟu virittaleṉpatu.
āṟuṟuppum pāviṉamuṅ kūṟuvaleṉṟu pukuntu poruḷārāyacci palavuṅ kūṟutal. ituvum iṉṉōraṉṉavum mikipaṭak kūṟalāy aṭaṅkumeṉiṉum amiyum.
12niṉṟupayaṉiṉmi yeṉpatu.
piṟkālattut tōṉṟiya vaḻakkēpaṟṟi avaṟṟiṟkuṅ kuṟṟantīra ilakkaṇaṅ kāṭṭiyavaḻi, atu cāṉṟōr ceyyuṭkup payaṉpaṭātu tammōraṉṉa ceyyuṭkup payaṉpaṭṭoḻiyac ceytal. avi vantavaḻik kaṇṭukoḷka. pāṭṭiyaṉ marapeṉak kāṭṭuvaṉavumavi.
13iṉi veṟṟeṉat toṭutta leṉpatu.
kēṭpōrk kiṉṉā yāppiṉpāṟ paṭṭu aṭaṅkum.
14ceṉṟutēyntiṟuta leṉpatu.
collappukuntatoru poruḷ ellāviṭattuñ collap paṭuvatākavum īṟṟukkaṇ māyntu māṟuvatu, atuvuṅ kuṉṟak kūṟalāy aṭaṅkum oḻintaṉavum aṉṉa.
maṟṟuk kūṟiyatukūṟaṉ muṉvittateṉṉiyeṉiṉ --atu nūluṭpōlac ceyyuṭkaṇṇum payaṉpaṭavariṉ amiyu meṉṟaṟ keṉpatu. avi:
vikalum vikal varakkaṇṭu maḵtuṇarār	(nālaṭi-4: 9)
eṉavum,
vañciyāy vañciyār kō	(yā-vi-pa; 230)
eṉavuñ colvakiyāṉ iraṭṭittavāṟu, iṉiyamivaṉa vaṟṟiṭiyē kuṉṟakkūṟal vittāṉ avaṟṟuttuṇic ciṟappilaveṉṟu aiyuṟāmi yeṉpatu.
ivvāciriyar yāṇṭum ilakkaṇamē kūṟi ivvilak kaṇattiṟ piṟaḻavarutalik kuṟṟameṉṟu koḷḷa lippiṉaṉṟi illāta kuṟṟaṅkaṭku ilakkaṇaṅ kūṟātār1 itu kūṟiṉār.
etirmaṟut tuṇariṉ ṟiṟattavu mavi	(664)
eṉa varukiṉṟa cūttirattāṉ iviyum ilakkaṇattiṟku upakārappaṭutali ṉeṉpatu2.
āyvuri :
iḵtu īriṅkuṟṟam (pattuk kuṟṟaṅkaḷ) iviyeṉak kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) nūṟkuc citiveṉak kūṟappaṭum kuṟṟaṅkaḷip paḻitīra ārāyiṉ, kūṟiyatukūṟal, māṟukoḷakkūṟal, kuṉṟak kūṟal, mikipaṭakkūṟal, poruḷilamoḻital, mayaṅkakkūṟal, kēṭpōrkku iṉṉā yāppiṟṟātal, paḻitta moḻiyāṉ iḻukkam kūṟal, taṉṉāṉ oruporuḷ karutikkūṟal, eṉṉa vakiyiṉum maṉaṅkōḷiṉmi eṉṉum ippattum avi pōlvaṉa piṟavum īriṅ kuṟṟam eṉa muṉṉart tokut turikkappaṭṭa akkuṟṟaṅkaḷiṉ viriyākum e-ṟu.	(110)
111.	etirmaṟut tuṇariṉat tiṟattavum aviyē.
iḷampūraṇam :
ituvumatu. nūṟkuṟṟam uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ) etirmaṟuttu uṇarvarāyiṉ, attiṟattavum kuṟṟamā meṉṟavāṟu.
pāvañ ceytāṉ niriyam pukumeṉak karutik kūṟuvāṉ tavañceyvāṉ cuvarkkam pukumeṉṟal. ivvāṟu kūṟic cuvarkkam peṟumeṉṉum poruṭ ... niriyampukumeṉṟa poruḷ tōṉṟāmiyiṟ kuṟṟamāyiṟṟu.
pērāciriyam :
itu, mēṟ kuṟṟam pattuṅ kūṟi iṉik kuṇamum pattuḷa veṉkiṉṟatu. mēlellām ivvāciriyaṉ ilakkaṇa vaḻakkiṉiyē vitantōti ataṉiṟpiṟaḻntatu kuṟṟameṉṟu koḷḷa vittāṉ; ivvōttiṉuḷ īriṅkuṟṟamu meṉpaṉa cila kuṟṟaṅ kūṟiṉāṉ1 ; itu māṟukoḷak kūṟalāṅ kollō veṉiṉ aṟṟaṉṟu; iviyum ilakkaṇamē kūṟiṉaṉeṉpāṉ ivaṟṟi yetirmaṟuttuk koḷḷa veṉṟāṉeṉpatu. itaṉatu payaṉ; uḷḷatu collutalēyaṉṟi illatu collutalum nūlilakkaṇameṉṟaṟivitta lāyiṟṟu; avi
peyarnilik kiḷavi kālan tōṉṟā
	(tol-col-vēṟ : 9)
eṉavum,
viṉiyeṉap paṭuvatu vēṟṟumi koḷḷātu
	(tol-col-viṉi: 1)
eṉavum varum. eṉavē, kūṟiyatu kūṟāmiyum māṟu koḷak kūṟāmiyuṅ kuṉṟakkūṟāmiyum pōlvaṉavum pattu uḷa nūlilakkaṇameṉṟāṉām. mēl,
īriṅ kuṟṟamumiṉṟi
eṉṟatallatu avaṟṟi nāṭṭikkoṇṭu etirmaṟuttuk kōṭal peṟāmiyiṉ itu kūṟiṉāṉeṉpatu.
āyvuri :
itu nūlukkuriya kuṇaṅkaḷāmāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i--ḷ) mēṟkuṟitta pattuk kuṟṟaṅkaḷiyum etir maṟuttu uṇarntu koḷḷutaṟkuriya kuṇaṅkaḷ pattum mēṟ kuṟitta nūlukkuriya ilakkaṇaṅkaḷām e-ṟu.
nūlukku amiya vēṇṭiyaṉavākavuḷḷaṉavaṟṟi eṭuttuk kūṟutalōṭu, nūluṭ pukutaṟku uriyaṉavaṟṟi eṭuttuk kūṟutalum nūṟkuriyatōr ilakkaṇamē eṉpataṉi aṟivuṟut tuvār nūlil iṭam peṟāta kuṟṟaṅkaḷiyum eṭuttuk kūṟiṉār. āciriyar. eṉavē, muṟkūṟiya pattuk kuṟṟaṅkaḷi etir maṟuttuk koḷḷappaṭum pattuk kuṇaṅkaḷum nūlukkuriya ilakkaṇamām eṉpār etir maṟuttuṇariṉ tiṟattavum aviyē eṉṟār. mēṟkuṟitta kuṟṟaṅkaḷiyetir maṟuttuk koḷḷutalāvatu, kūṟiyatu kūṟāmi, māṟukoḷak kūṟāmi kuṉṟak kūṟāmi, mikipaṭak kūṟāmi, poruḷila moḻi yāmi, mayaṅkakkūṟāmi, kēṭpōrkku iṉṉā yāppiṉi yuṭiyatākāmi, paḻitta moḻiyāṉ iḻukkam kūṟāmi, taṉṉāṉ oru poruḷ karutik kūṟāmi, eṉṉavakiyiṉum maṉaṅ kōḷiṉmiyillāmi eṉa ivvāṟu koḷḷutal.
		curuṅkac collal viḷaṅka vittal
naviṉṟōrk kiṉimi naṉmoḻi puṇarttal
ōci yuṭimi āḻamuṭit tātal
muṟiyil vippē ulakamaliyāmi
viḻumiyatu payattal viḷaṅkutāraṇatta
tākutal nūliṟ kaḻakeṉum pattē
eṉa varum naṉṉūl-pāyiram-mayili nātar urimēṟ kōṭ cūttiram nūlukkuriya kuṇaṅkaḷākiya pattaḻakiṉiyum virittuk kūṟuvatākum.	(111)
112.	otta kāṭci uttivaki virippiṉ
nutaliya taṟital atikāra muṟiyē
tokuttuk kūṟal vakuttumeyn niṟuttal
moḻinta poruḷō ṭoṉṟavittal
moḻiyā tataṉi muṭṭiṉṟi muṭittal
vārā tataṉāṉ vantatu muṭittal
vantatu koṇṭu vārātatu muṭittal
muntu moḻintataṉ talitaṭu māṟṟē
oppak kūṟal orutali moḻiyē
taṉkōṭ kūṟal uṭampoṭu puṇarttal
piṟaṉuṭam paṭṭatu tāṉuṭam paṭutal
iṟantatu kāttal etiratu pōṟṟal
moḻivām eṉṟal kūṟiṟ ṟeṉṟal
tāṉkuṟi yiṭutal orutali yaṉmi
muṭintatu kāṭṭal āṇi kūṟal
palporuṭ kēṟpiṉnallatu kōṭal
tokutta moḻiyāṉ vakuttaṉar kōṭal
maṟutali citittut taṉtuṇi purittal
piṟaṉkōṭ kūṟal aṟiyā tuṭampaṭal
poruḷiṭi yiṭutal etirporuḷa uṇarttal
colliṉ eccam colliyāṅ kuṇarttal
tantupuṇarn turittal ñāpakam kūṟal
uyttukkoṇ ṭuṇarttaloṭu meyppaṭa nāṭic
colliya alla piṟavavaṇvariṉum
colliya vakiyāṟ curuṅka nāṭi
maṉatti ṉeṇṇi mācaṟat terintu koṇṭu
iṉattiṟ cērtti uṇarttal vēṇṭum
nuṉittaku pulavar kūṟiya nūlē.
iḷampūraṇam :
tantiravutti yāmā ṟuṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
itaṉ poḻippu : nutaliya taṟital mutalākac collap paṭṭaṉavum attaṉmiya piṟavun tantira uttiyām eṉṟavāṟu.
tantirameṉiṉum nūleṉiṉum okkum. uttiyeṉpatu vaṭa moḻic citivu. atu cūttirattiṉpāṟ kiṭappatōr poruḷ vēṟupāṭu kāṭṭuvatu.
(i-ḷ.) otta kāṭci uttivaki virippiṉeṉpatu nūṟkup poruntiya kāṭciyiṉā ṉurikkum uttivakiyi virikkuṅkālat teṉṟavāṟu.
nutaliyataṟitalāvatu --- cūttirattiṟ coṟṟa poruḷuṇart talaṉṟi, itaṉkarut tituveṉa uṇarttal.
aḵtāvatu eḻutteṉappaṭupa (nūṉmarapu : 1) eṉṉuñ cūttirattuḷ eḻuttu iṉitteṉa variyaṟuttuṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu eṉṟal.
atikāra muṟiyāvatu muṉṉam palaporuḷi yatikaritta vaḻip piṉṉum ammuṟiyiṉāṉē virittuṇarttutal.
aḵtāvatu, uyartiṇi yaḵṟiṇiyeṉa atikarittu āṭūu vaṟicol (kiḷaviyākkam - 2) eṉṉuñ cūttirattāṉ niṟuttamuṟi piṟaḻāmal uyartiṇikūṟal. iṉṉum itaṉāṉē oru cūttirattilē karutiṉa poruḷi vittu varukiṉṟa cūttirattuḷ ōtātu ataṉ kāriyamāyiṉa kūṟiyavaḻi ataṉic cūttirantōṟuṅ koṇarnturittal. aḵtāvatu akara viṟutip peyarnili muṉṉar (uyirmayaṅkiyal-1) eṉṉuñ cūttirattiṟ kacatapat tōṉṟi ṉeṉavōti viṉiyeñcu kiḷaviyu, (uyirmaṅkiyal - 2) meṉṉuñ cūttirattuḷ ōtiṟṟila rāyiṉum. atikayāramuṟimiyiṉāṉ valleḻuttuvaruvaḻi yeṉa vurittal
tokuttuk kūṟalāvatu---vakipeṟak kūṟal vēṇṭu māyiṉum ataṉit tokuttuk kūṟal.
eḻutteṉap paṭupa akaramuta ṉakara viṟuvāymuppaḵ teṉpa (nūṉ marapu, 1) eṉṟāṟpōlvaṉa iṉṉum pala cūttirattāṟ kūṟiya poruḷi ittuṇiyuṅ kūṟappaṭṭatitu veṉak kūṟalumām. tūkkiyal vakiyē yāṅkeṉa moḻipa (ceyyuḷiyal-83) eṉpataṉāṟ koḷka.
vakuttu meynniṟut talāvatu---tokipaṭak kūṟiya poruḷi vakipaṭak kūṟal.
atu a, i, u, e, o (nūṉmarapu, 3) eṉṉuñ cūttirattāṟ koḷka. iṉṉumataṉāṉē tokipaṭac cūttirañceyta vaḻi avaṟṟuḷ orōvoṉṟu potuvilakkaṇattāṉ muṭiyātavaḻip perumpāṉmi ciṟupāṉmikoṇṭu vakuttup poruḷurit talumām. iṉṉumataṉāṉē tokipaṭak kūṟiyataṉi vakuttup poruḷurittalumām.
moḻinta poruḷōṭoṉṟavitta lāvatu --- cūttirattuṭporuḷ palapaṭat tōṉṟumāyiṉ muṟpaṭṭa cūttirat tiṟkokkum poruḷurittal.
aṉṟiyum muṟpaṭṭa cūttirattiṉāṉ oruporuḷōtiya vaḻip piṟpaṭṭa cūttiramum poruḷōṭoṉṟavittalumām.
moḻiyā tataṉi muṭṭiṉṟi muṭittalāvatu---eṭut tōtāta poruḷi muṭṭuppaṭāmal uriyiṉāṉ muṭittal.
itaṉi uriyiṟkōṭal eṉpa. ikkaruttiṉāṉē,
cūttirat tuṭporu ḷaṉṟiyum yāppuṟavu
iṉṟi yamiyā tiyipavi yellām
oṉṟa vurippa turiyeṉap paṭumē.	(marapiyal-105)
eṉa ōtuvārāyiṟṟeṉka
vārā tataṉāṉ vantatu muṭitta lāvatu---oruṅkeṇ ṇappaṭṭa poruḷoṉṟaṉip pakuttuk kūṟiyavaḻi āṇṭu vārātataṟkōtiya vilakkaṇatti itaṉkaṇṇum varuvit tuṇarttutal.
vantatukoṇṭu vārātatu muṭittalāvatu---oruṅ keṇṇappaṭṭavaṟṟuḷoṉṟip pakuttu ilakkaṇaṅ kūṟiya vaḻi vārātataṉkaṇṇum ivvilakkaṇattik kūṭṭimuṭittal.
muntu moḻintataṉ talitaṭumāṟ ṟāvatu --- muṟpaṭa atikaritta poruḷi yavvakiyiṉāṟ kūṟātu muṟipiṟaḻak kūṟutal.
ivvāṟu kūṟuṅkāl orupayaṉōkkik kūṟal vēṇṭum atu puḷḷimayaṅkiyaluṭ kaṇṭukoḷka.
oppakkūṟaleṉpatu---oru poruḷeṭuttu ilakkaṇaṅ kūṟiya vaḻi atupōlvaṉavaṟṟiyu milakkaṇattāṉ muṭittal.
orutalimoḻiyāvatu---ēkākkara meṉṉum vaṭa moḻip poruṇmi. aḵtāvatu, cūttirattiṟkup poruḷ kavarttut tōṉṟiṉ ataṉuḷoṉṟaṉit tuṇintu kūṟal.
taṉkōṭ kūṟalāvatu --- piṟanūlāciriyar kūṟiyavāṟu kūṟātu taṉkōṭpāṭṭāl kūṟutal.
atu vēṟṟumi eṭṭeṉṟal.
uṭampoṭu puṇarttalāvatu --- ilakkaṇa vakiyāṉ ōtutalaṉṟi yāciriyaṉukkiṉṟic cūttirattiṉkaṇṇē yoru colli vippaṉāyiṉ avvippiṉi ilakkaṇamākak kōṭal.
oṟṟīṟṟuc colli yukaraṅkoṭuttuk kūṟukaveṉa vilakkaṇaṅ kūṟiṟṟilarāyiṉum ārum aruvum īroṭu civaṇum viḷi marapu (21) eṉa ōtutaliṉ, ār eṉpatu ārum eṉa ukaram peṟṟatu. itaṉip piṟāṇṭuṅ kōṭal.
piṟaṉuṭam paṭṭatu tāṉuṭampaṭutalāvatu --- piṟa nūlāciri yaṉ  uṭampaṭṭa poruṭkut tāṉuṭampaṭutal.
aḵtāvatu iraṇṭām vēṟṟumi ceyappaṭuporuṭ kaṇ varumeṉap pāṇiṉiyār ōtiṉār ; aḵtu ivarkkum uṭampāṭu.
iṟantatu kāttalāvatu --- mēṟkūṟappaṭṭa cūttirattāṟ kūṟappaṭāta poruḷip piṉvarukiṉṟa cūttirattā ṉamittal.
etiratu pōṟṟalāvatu --- muṉ kūṟappaṭṭa cūttirat tāṉē varukiṉṟa cūttirattiṟ poruḷiṉiyum pātukākkumāṟu vittal.
moḻivāmeṉṟalāvatu --- cila poruḷik kūṟi avaṟṟuḷoṉṟaṉi yiṉṉaviṭattuk kūṟuvāmeṉa vurittal.
puṇariya ṉiliyiṭik kuṟukalum	(moḻimarapu. 2)
eṉpataṉāṟ koḷka.
kūṟiṟṟeṉṟalāvatu --- pala poruḷā yatikarittavaṟṟuṭ cila poruḷi mēṟcollappaṭṭaṉaveṉṟal.
māttiri vakiyum eḻuttiyal vakiyu mēṟkiḷan taṉṉa (ceyyuḷiyal.2) eṉṟataṉāṟ koḷka.
tāṉkuṟiyiṭutalāvatu --- ulakiṉkaṇ vaḻakkiṉṟi yoru poruṭku āciriyaṉṟāṉ kuṟiyiṭal.
aḵtu uyartiṇi yaḵṟiṇiyeṉpaṉa.
orutaliyaṉmi muṭintatu kāṭṭalāvatu oru poruḷi yōtiya vaḻic colluvataṟkē yurittaṉṟip piṟa poruṭkum potuvāka muṭittami kāṭṭal.
āṇi kūṟalāvatu --- oru poruḷik kūṟumvaḻi ētuviṉāṟ kūṟalaṉṟit taṉ ṉāṇiyāṟ kūṟal.
vēṟṟumi yēḻeṉap pāṇiṉiyār kūṟiṉamiyiṉ avar viḷiyi mutalvēṟṟumiyi laṭakkiṉār. ataṟkut tiripukūṟātu ataṉi eṭṭām vēṟṟumiyeṉṟal āṇṭuk kaṭāvappaṭā.
palporuṭ kēṟpiṉ nallatu kōṭalāvatu---oru cūttiram palaporuṭ kēṟkumāyiṉ avaṟṟuḷ nallataṉip poruḷākak kōṭal.
tokutta moḻiyāṉ vakuttaṉar kōṭalāvatu---tokuttuk kūṟiya col taṉṉāṉē piṟitumoru poruḷ vakuttukkāṭṭal.
atu kuṟṟiyalukā muṟippeyar maruṅkiṉ	(viḷimarapu, 9)
eṉṉuñ cūttirattāṉ moḻimutaṟ kuṟṟukaramuṅ kōṭal.
colliṉ muṭipiṉ apporuṇ muṭittaleṉpatumatu.
maṟutali citittut taṉṟuṇi purittalāvatu---piṟa nūlāciriyaṉ kūṟiṉa poruṇmiyik keṭuttut taṉṟuṇivu kūṟutal.
aḵtāvatu neṭṭeḻuttēḻ aḷapeṭiyeṉpaṉa kuṟ ṟeḻuttiṉ vikārameṉpāri maṟuttu vēṟōreḻuttāka vōtutal.
piṟaṉkōṭ kūṟalāvatu---piṟa nūlāciriyaṉ koṇṭa kōṭpāṭṭik kūṟutal.
atu vēṟṟumi tāmē yēḻeṉa moḻipa
	(vēṟṟumi yiyal. 1) eṉṟal.
aṟiyā tuṭampaṭalāvatu---tāṉaṟiyāta poruḷip piṟar kūṟiyavāṟṟā ṉuṭampaṭutal.
atu ēḻānarakam ittaṉmit teṉa voruvaṉ kūṟiyavaḻi atu pulaṉākātātaliṉ avaṉ coṉṉataṟ kuṭampaṭutal; itu vaḻinūlāciriyark kurittu.
poruḷiṭi yiṭutalāvatu---oruporuḷi yōtiya vaḻiyataṟkiṉamākiya poruḷic cērakkūṟātu iṭiyīṭupaṭak kūṟutal.
atu peṇmi cuṭṭiya (peyariyal. 24) veṉṉuñ cūttiramōti ataṉ pakutiyākiya āṇmi tirinta peyar nilik kiḷavi yeṉpataṉi iṭiyiṭṭu vittal pōlvaṉa.
etirporu ḷuṇarttalāvatu --- iṉik kūṟavēṇṭuvatitu veṉavuṇarttal.
colliṉ eccam colliyāṅ kuṇarttaleṉpatu --- pirinili mutalākac collappaṭṭa eccaṅkaḷik kaṇṭu āṅkuc colliyavāṟṟāṟ poruḷkōṭal.
tantupuṇarnturittalāvatu --- muṉṉāyiṉum piṉṉā yiṉum niṉṟa cūttirattiṟ colli iṭiniṉṟa cūttirattiṉuṅ koṇarntu puṇarnturittal.
ñāpakaṅ kūṟalāvatu --- iraṭṭuṟamoḻintu iraṇṭu coṟkum poruḷkōṭal.
uyttukkoṇṭuṇartalāvatu --- oru cūttirattāṉ ōrilakkaṇam ōtiyavaḻi ataṟkup poruntāmi yuḷatākat tōṉṟiṉ ataṟkup poruntumāṟu vicārittuṇartal
paṉiyeṉṉuñ collukku attum iṉṉuñ cāriyiyā meṉṟārāyiṉum (uyirmayaṅkiyal 39) avaṟṟuḷ eṟpa toṉṟātaliṉ iṉṉīṟṟāyavāṟu varuvaṉa vuyttuṇartalām.
ivi muppattiraṇṭun tantiravuttiyāvaṉa.

pōraciriyam :
itu, muṟiyāṉē iṟutikkaṇiṉṟa muppattiruvaki yuttiyuṅ kūṟi maṟṟum innūluḷ atikāram mūṉṟaṟkum vēṇṭum puṟaṉaṭiyuṅ kūṟutaṉutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ) : ottakāṭci yuttivaki virippiṉ---muṟkūṟiya kuṟṟaṅkaḷōṭu oppattōṉṟun tōṟṟattiṉi uṭiyavākiya uttik kūṟṟiṉi virittuc colliṉ.
pattuvakik kuṟṟattōṭum ottuvarumeṉavē iviyum nūṟkaṇaṉṟi oḻinta ceyyuṭku varuṅkāl vilakkappaṭutalum. muṟkūṟiya kuṟṟampōla iviyum vēṟu cila poruḷ paṭittalumuṭiyavāyiṉa. kāṭciyutti yeṉṟu ivaṟṟik kūṟiyavataṉāṉ nūluṭ kāṇappaṭumeṉṟa aintu kuṟṟattōṭum ottal koḷḷappaṭum; avvannūṟkup payampaṭavarum pakutiyāṉeṉpatu.
utti yeṉpatu, nūlceyyuṅkāl iyalpuvakiyākiya vaḻakkuñ ceyyuḷum pōlac cevvaṉañ collutal oṇmi  yuṭittaṉṟām piṟaveṉiṉ aṟṟaṉṟu; avvāṟu ceytakkāl,
nuṇmiyoṭu puṇarnta oṇmittākal vēṇṭum			(655) 
eṉpatu muṉṉarccolliṉāṉ. īṇṭuc cevvaṉañ collāta tantiravutti vakiyum avvāṟē oṇmiyuṭiya vāmeṉpatu karuttu; eṉṟārkkuc cevvaṉañ ceytali uttiyeṉṉāṉō veṉiṉ-atu collāmi  muṭintatākali ṉaṉṟē utti yeṉṉātu ivaṟṟi uttivakiyeṉpāṉāyiṟ ṟeṉpatu; aḵtēl, ivaṟṟi muṉ tokuttāṉpōla virippiṉeṉṟa teṉṉiyeṉiṉ muṉṉar etiratunōkki (marapiyal :98) muppattiraṇṭeṉat tokikūṟippiṉṉar,
		maṉatti ṉeṇṇi mācaṟat terintukoṇṭu
iṉattiṟcērtti yuṇarttal vēṇṭum
eṉkiṉṟāṉākalāṉ, aṅṅaṉam iṉampaṟṟi avaṟṟōṭu aṭaṅkuvaṉavellām avaṟṟu viriyātumeṉṉuṅ karuttiṉāṟ kūṟiṉāṉeṉpatu.
		avaṉiva ṉuvaṉeṉa varūum peyaru
mavaḷiva ḷuvaḷeṉa varūum peyaru
mavariva ruvareṉa varūum peyarum
yāṉyā nāmeṉa varūum peyarum
yāvaṉ yāvaḷ yāvareṉṉu
māvayiṉ mūṉṟō ṭappati ṉintum
pālaṟi vanta vuyartiṇip peyarē
	(tol col-peyar :8)
eṉpataṉuḷ,
āvayiṉ mūṉṟō ṭappati ṉintum
eṉat tokuttuk kūṟiyavaḻi atu mikipaṭak kūṟalākātu; eṉṉi? ummiyeṇṇākaliṉ tokiyiṉṟiyuñ cilvaki yeḻuttiṟ ceyyuṭṭāka vaḻakkiyalāṉē curuṅkacceyvatōr āṟuḷatāyiṉum appatiṉintumeṉat tokuttuk kūṟal uttivakiyāṉ amiyumākaliṉeṉpatu karuttu. iṅṅaṉan tokikūṟutal veḷḷitaṉṟi oḷḷitākal; avi, patiṉintu peyarumē orunikaraṉaveṉpatu aṟiyalākumeṉpatu.
iṉi,
meypeṟu marapiṟ ṟoṭivaki tāmē
	(tol-cey :101)
eṉṉuñ cūttirampōlvaṉa piṟitoruporuḷ payakku meṉṟalum accūttirattuṭ kāṭṭappaṭṭatu. ivvāṟṟāṉ ivvuttivaki ellā nūṟkum iṉṟiyamiyāvāyiṉa.
maṟṟu, vakiyeṉappaṭātu cevvaṉañceyyum utti yāvaṉa yāviyeṉṉiṉ.
iyaṟkip poruḷi yiṟṟeṉak kiḷattal
	(tol-col-kiḷa :19)
eṉṟāṟpōlac colliyoḻivaṉa, piṟavum aṉṉa.
virippi ṉeṉṟa viṉiyeccañ cērtti yuṇarttal vēṇṭu meṉṟa muṟṟuviṉikoṇṭu muṭintatu.  meyppaṭa nāṭi yeṉpaṉa pōlvaṉavaṟṟukkum ituvē muṭipu; ataṟku nūleṉṉum peyarkoṭuttu muṭikka, nūliyeṉṟu aikāram virippiṉ, vēṇṭumeṉpatu taṉpālāṉum piṟaṉpālāṉum peṟṟatōr peyarkoṇṭu muṭiyum.
(1)	nutaliyataṟital---cūttirattuḷ ōtiya poruḷāṟ collappaṭum payaṉ illatupōlac colliyataṉāṉē ataṟkēṟṟavakiyāṟ karutiyuṇarappaṭuporuḷ iṉṉateṉṟu koḷḷa vittal: atu,
vēṟuviṉip potucco loruviṉi kiḷavār
	(tol-col-kiḷavi :46)
eṉavum,
		ciṟappi ṉākiya peyarnilik kiḷavikku
miyaṟpeyark kiḷavi muṟpaṭak kiḷavār
	(tol-col-kiḷavi :41)
eṉavum ivi vaḻuvaṟka vaḻuvamikka eṉṉum irupakutiyuḷ vaḻuvamittaṟkeḻuntaṉaveṉpatu karutiṉāṉ  āciriyaṉeṉpatu aṟiyavantaṉa. itaṉi mutaṟkaṇ vittuppōy iṟutik kaṇṇē uyttukkoṇṭuṇartali vittāṉ ituvum atupōl uyttukkoṇṭuṇartal oruvakiyāṉuṭit tāyiṉum ataṉāṟ peṟṟa payaṉ piṟitumoṉṟu uḷatātal vēṟṟumi yuṭimiyiṉeṉpatu; atu muṉṉarccollutum.
iṉi, mūvakit tamiḻvaḻakkamum nutaliyatum avaṟṟuḷ iyaṟṟamiḻē nutaliyatum, atikāramnutaliyatum, atikārattuḷ ottu nutaliyatum, ōttiṉuṭ cūttiram nutaliyatumeṉavum ivi nutaliyataṟitaṟ pakutiyāy aṭaṅkumeṉpāṉ 
		maṉatti ṉeṇṇi mācaṟat terintukoṇṭu
iṉattiṟ cērtti yuṇarttal vēṇṭum
eṉkiṉṟāṉeṉpatu, mēlvarukiṉṟavaṟṟukkumokkum. aḵtēl itaṉi āṇṭuvittu maṟṟataṉi īṇṭu vippiṉum iḵtokkum piṟaveṉiṉ, aṟṟaṉṟu; nūliṉvēṟākiya pāyirattuḷḷāyiṉum innutaliyataṟital varutalum utti yeṉṟaṟku itaṉi muṉvittāṉeṉpatu.
2)	atikā ramuṟimi --- eṉpatu muṉṉiṉṟa cūttirap poruṇmi piṉvaruñ cūttirattiṟkum peṟaṟpālaṉa peṟa vittal;avi,
iyaṟkip poruḷi yiṟṟeṉak kiḷattal
	(tol-col-kiḷa :19)
eṉṟavaḻi iṟṟeṉak kiḷatta lurimipūṇṭa taṉṟē, ataṉic 
ceyaṟkip poruḷi yākkamoṭu kūṟal
	(tol-col-kiḷa:20)
eṉpuḻiyuṅ koḷḷa vittalum,
kuṟṟiya likara niṟṟalvēṇṭum	(tol-eḻut-moḻi 1)
eṉpataṉik, 
kuṟṟiya lukaram vallā ṟūrntu	(tol-eḻut-moḻi 3)
niṟṟal vēṇṭum eṉṟu koḷḷa vittalum pōlvaṉa.
iṉi, vaḻakkiyalum vaḻakkiyalāṟ ceyyappaṭṭa cey yuḷiyalum paṟṟi eḻunta ilakkaṇam iyaṟṟamiḻeṉappaṭum. acceyyuḷiṉṟi amiyāta iciyilakkaṇam icittami ḻeṉap peyareyti avviyaṟṟamiḻp piṉṉar vikkap paṭṭateṉappaṭum; ivviraṇṭaṉvaḻi nikaḻttuṅ kūttilakkaṇaṅ kūṟiya nāṭakat tamiḻ avaṟṟup piṉṉarttāmeṉa muṟimi kūṟutalum; iṉi iyaṟṟamiḻuḷḷum eḻuttatikārat tōṭu collatikārattiṟkuñ collatikārattōṭu poruḷati kārattiṟkum iyiyu kūṟutalum atikārattuḷ ōttuppala vākaliṉ avi oṉṟaṉpiṉṉoṉṟu vittaṟku iyipu kūṟutalum avvāṟē cūttirattiṟku iyipu kūṟutalu mellām atikāra muṟimikku iṉameṉṟu cērtti yuṇarappaṭum. piṟavum aṉṉa
atikārameṉṟa poruṇmi yeṉṉiyeṉiṉ, muṟimi yeṉavum, iṭameṉavuṅ, kiḻamiyeṉavuṅ koḷḷappaṭum. avaṟṟuḷ, īṇṭu atikārameṉṟoḻiyātu muṟimi yeṉavuṅ kūṟiṉamiyiṉ atikārameṉpatu muṟimip poruṭṭaṉṟi muṉ ōriṭattu niṟutti ataṉ vaḻimuṟiyāṟ poruḷ kōṭal koḷka; eṉṉi? muṉṉum piṉṉum niṉṟa cūttiram iraṇṭaṉiyum ōriṭattaṉavākak karutal vēṇṭum; aṅṅaṉaṅ karutaliyipukoṇṭaṉṟi oṉṟaṉ poruṇmi oṉṟaṟku varuvittal aritākalāṉum aṅṅaṉam iṭam paṟṟākkāl oruvaṉ ceyta nūloṭu piṟatorunūṟku iyipu kūṟātavāṟupōla eḻuttatikārattōṭu collatikārattiṭi iyipu kūṟalvēṇṭu vataṉṟāvāṉ cellumātalāṉu meṉpatu. eṉavē, iṭameṉappaṭṭatutāṉē muṟimip poruḷum paṭumāyiṉum iccūttirattuḷ atikārameṉavum muṟimiyeṉavuṅ kūṟiṉamiyiṉ īṇṭu iṭamuṟimi yeṉpatē karuttāyiṟṟu. itaṉāṉē uṭaṉ piṟantāruḷ oruvaṟkuriyatu vaḻittōṉṟiṉārkkum oruvaḻi uriyavāṟupōla muṉṉar niṉṟa viti piṉṉar vantataṟkum vēṇṭiyavaḻik koḷḷappaṭumeṉpatu uttivakiyāyiṟṟu.
iṉiyorucārār, iṅṅaṉam varintukoṇṭa iṭattuḷḷē yāṟṟoḻukkup pōlavaṉṟi iṭiyiṭiyum peṟu meṉpatu nōkki arimā nōkkun, tērip pāyttuḷum, paruntu viḻukkāṭum āki varumeṉavuñ collupa; aviyum iṉattiṟ cērtti yuṇarttavēpaṭuvameṉpatu.
(3)	tokuttut kūṟal --- tokuttiyātta nūluḷḷun tokuttukkūṟutal :
aviyāvaṉa : eḻuttu muppattumūṉṟu, colliraṇṭu, poruḷiraṇṭu eṉāciriyaṉṟāṉē toki kūṟutal pōl vaṉa
		irutiṇi maruṅki ṉimpā laṟiya
vīṟṟiṉiṉ ṟicikkum patiṉō reḻuttum
	(tol-col-kiḷavi : 10)
eṉap pala cūttirattu virintaṉa tokuttalum,
		āvayiṉ mūṉṟō ṭappati ṉintum
pālaṟi vanta vuyartiṇip peyar
	(tol-col-peya : 8)
eṉa oru cūttirattu virittut tokuttalum pōlvaṉavum ataṉ pakutiyāy aṭaṅkum.
(4)	vakuttu meynniṟuttal aṅṅaṉan tokuttukkūṟiya vaḻi eḻuttu muppattumūṉṟeṉṟa tokiyiṉik kuṟṟeḻuttum neṭṭeḻuttum uyirum uyirmeyyum valliṉamum melliṉamum iṭiyiṉamu meṉṟāṟ pōlavum uyartiṇi aḵṟiṇi yeṉṟa tokiyiṉi oruvaṉ orutti palar oṉṟu palaveṉṟāṟ pōlavum vakuttal:
		uyartiṇik kurimiyu maḵṟiṇik kurimiyu
māyiru tiṇikkumō raṉṉa vurimiyum
	(tol-col-peya: 6)
eṉavum,
		iyaṟco ṟirico ṟiciccol vaṭacoleṉ
ṟaṉittē ceyyu ḷīṭṭac collē
		(tol-col-ec: 1)
eṉavum varuvaṉavum avi ivaṟṟuḷ irutiṇiyeṉak kūṟip peyar mūṉṟu, viṉi mūṉṟeṉṟalum; iṉi akattiṇi puṟattiṇiyeṉakkūṟi, akampuṟameṉak kōṭalum iṉa meṉṟatu; avi kuṟṟeḻuttu neṭṭeḻutteṉa muṟkūṟi yaṉavē maṟṟoru peyar peṟṟāṟpōlātu colvakiyāṉ mūṉṟāvatōr col vēṟu peṟṟamiyiṉeṉpatu. iṅṅaṉaṅ koḷḷākkāl irutiṇip peyarum viṉiyum mummūṉ ṟeṉal ākātaṉṟōveṉpatu
		colleṉap paṭupa peyarē viṉiyeṉ
ṟāyiraṇ ṭeṉpa vaṟintici ṉōrē
	(tol.col-peya: 4)
eṉṟavaḻi,
		iṭiccoṟ kiḷaviyu muriccoṟ kiḷaviyu
mavaṟṟuvaḻi maruṅkiṟ ṟōṉṟu meṉpa
eṉa vakuttalum, iṉi ārāynta iyaṟcolloṭu vēṟu mūṉṟu coṟkūṭṭi nāṉkeṉṟalum avvāṟē iṉameṉap paṭum. eṉṉi? oḻinta mūṉṟu collum iyaṟcolliṉ vēṟupaṭṭami kūṟumākali ṉeṉpatu. aḵtēl, eḻuttu muppattumūṉṟeṉak kūṟip piṉṉar irunūṟṟorupat tāṟuyir meyk kuppi kōṭalum vēṟṟeḻuttāki varu meṉpatu vakuttu meynniṟuttaleṉpataṉāṟ koḷḷāmō veṉiṉ kōṭumaṉṟē;
puḷḷi yillā vellā meyyum
eṉpuḻi,
uruvuru vāki yakaramo ṭuyirttalum
eṉa āciriyaṉ iraṇṭeḻuttiṉ kūṭṭamākac cūttirañ ceytilāṉāyiṉeṉpatu.
meyyeṉṟataṉāṉ vakuttavaṟṟip piṉṉum vakuttu niṟuttal koḷḷappaṭum; eṉṉi? iyaṟcīrpatteṉavum āciriyavuriccīrāṟeṉavum vakuttavaṟṟi,
		iyaṟcī riṟuti nērava ṇiṟpi
ṉuriccīr veṇpā vāku meṉpa	(tol.cey: 19)
eṉa iyalacimayakkamākiya nāṉkaṉiyē iyaṟcīreṉavum,
		veṇpā vuriccī rāciriya vuriccī
riṉpā nēraṭik koruṅkunili yilavē
	(tol-cey: 23)
eṉṟavaḻi, āciriyavuriccīr āṟaṉuḷ iraṇṭaṉi oḻittu uriyaci mayakkamākiya nāṉkaṉiyē āciriyavuriccīreṉavum vakuttu niṉṟamiyiṉeṉpatu.
mey yeṉṟataṉāṉ vakuttavaṟṟip piṉṉum vakuttu niṟuttal koḷḷappaṭum; eṉṉi?
		iyalaci mayakka miyaṟcī rēṉi
yuriyaci mayakka māciriya vuriccīr	(tol-cey : 13)
eṉṟu pakuttōtiyavāṟṟāṉeṉpatu; akkaruttiṉāṉaṉṟē oḻinta iraṇṭaṉiyuṅ,
kalittaḷi maruṅkiṟ kaṭiyavum paṭāa
	(tol-cey : 24)
eṉa iṟantatutaḻīi urippāṉāyiṟṟeṉpatu.
(5)	moḻinta poruḷō ṭoṉṟa vavvayiṉ moḻiyātataṉi muṭṭiṉṟu muṭittal --- eṭuttōtiya poruṇmikku ēṟṟa vakiyāṉ apporuṇmikkaṭ  collātatoṉṟu koḷḷavittal.
muṭṭiṉṟi muṭittaleṉṟataṉāṉ eṭuttōtātum eṭuttōtiyataṉōṭu okkuñ ciṟappiṟṟeṉṟavāṟām. atu pōlvaṉa ataṟku iṉameṉappaṭum maṟṟu itaṉi aruttāpatti yeṉṉāmōveṉiṉ, eṉṉāmaṉṟē :
piṟacī ruḷvaḻit taṉṟaḷi vēṇṭupa
eṉṉum poruṭṭaṉṟit taṉcīroṭu iyaṟcīr vantu taḷi koḷḷumeṉa moḻiyātōr poruḷkōṭaliṉeṉka,
iṉi,
raḵkā ṉoṟṟum pakara viṟutiyum	(tol-col : 7)
eṉṟavaḻi oḻinta nāṉkeḻuttum īṟṟiṉiṟkumeṉṟu kōṭal pōlvaṉavum ataṉ iṉameṉappaṭum. eṉṉi? oṉṟeṉa muṭitta ṟaṉṉiṉa muṭitta leṉiṉum iḻukkātu. iṉic,
cīriyi maruṅki ṉōraci yoppiṉ
	(tol-col : 56)
eṉṟavaḻi, oṉṟātatu iyaṟcīrveṇṭaḷi yeṉṟukōṭal aruttāpattiyāki,
eṭutta moḻiyiṉañ ceppalu murittu
	(tol-cey : 61)
eṉṟa vaḻakkiyalāṉē vaḻuvaṉṟi aṭaṅkumeṉpatu.
(6)	vārātataṉāṉ vantatu muṭittal---oru poruṇ mikku vēṇṭum ilakkaṇam nirampa vārātatōr cūttirat tāṉē aṅṅaṉam vanta poruṇmikku vēṇṭum muṭipu koḷḷacceytal; atu,
		toṭara liṟuti tammuṟ ṟāmvariṉ
lakaram ṟakaravoṟ ṟākalu murittē
	(tol-eḻut-uyir : 12)
eṉa ataṟkuk kēṭu vārāta cūttirattāṉē lakaram ṟakara voṟṟāy varumeṉap pirittut tiripu kūṟiyatē paṟṟāka lakaram āṇṭu nillātu keṭumeṉṟu koḷḷavittataṉāṉē ciṟcila vittip paṟpala koṇṭāreṉṟu vanta puṇarcci muṭittavāṟu kaṇṭukoḷka.
		ellā meṉṉu miṟuti muṉṉar
vaṟṟeṉ cāriyi muṟṟat tōṉṟum
	(tol-eḻut-uru : 17)
eṉṟavaḻi, ellāmeṉṉum viravuppeyaruḷ uyartiṇi kūṟivaṟṟuccāriyi peṟāmiyāṉ atu peṟṟuvanta aḵṟiṇik kūṟē muṭittalum atu; piṟavum aṉṉa.
iṉi, iṅṅaṉam muṭipukōṭalaṉṟi āṇṭuk kuṟiyiṭutalum āṭciyuṅ kuṟiyīṭum oruṅkunikaḻntatu piṉṉar āṭcik kaṇ vārāmiyum vantavaḻip piṟavaṟṟōṭu kūṟutalum ataṟku iṉameṉappaṭum. avi,
avvaki yoṉpatum viṉiyeñcu kiḷavi
	(tol-col-viṉi : 31)
eṉa muṭintataṉi mīṇṭum,
neṟippaṭat tōṉṟu meñcuporuṭ kiḷavi
	(tol-col-ecca : 34)
eṉak kuṟiyiṭutal eccaviyaluḷ vantatāyiṉum muṉṉar ak kuṟiyīṭu vantatiṉmiyiṉ atu vārātateṉappaṭumākaliṉ aḵtu īṇṭuk kuṟiyiṭutalāyiṟṟu. viṉiyiyaluṭ peyarecca meṉṟu āḷutalum atu.
vaṇṇac ciṉiccol muṟṟuccol eṉpaṉavuṅ kuṟṟiyalikarattip puḷḷiyeṉṟalum āṭciyuṅ kuṟiyīṭum oruṅku nikaḻntaṉavākaliṉ aviyum iṉi vārāmiyāṉ vantuḻi  vantuḻi avvāṟu āṇṭāṉeṉpatu.
iṉip, puḷḷiyeṉa mēl āḷavārātataṉip puḷḷi yeṉṟu āḷvaṉavaṟṟoṭu mayakkaṅ kūṟutaleṉpatu,
		avitāṅ,
kuṟṟiya vikaraṅ kuṟṟiya lukara
māyta meṉṟu
muppāṟ puḷḷiyu meḻuttō raṉṉa
	(tol-eḻut-nūṉ : 2)
eṉpuḻik kuṟṟiyavikaram puḷḷiyeṉṟu yāṇṭum āḷavārā miyālum atutāṉ avvaḻi varavēṇṭutalāṉum aṅṅaṉam puḷḷiyeṉṟu āḷavaruṅ kuṟṟukarattōṭum āytattōṭum uṭaṉkūṟutalāyiṟṟu. iṅṅaṉam uṭaṉkūṟākkāṟ puḷḷiyuṅ kuṟṟikaramumeṉac cūttirampeṟutal vēṇṭuvatāvāṉ cellu meṉpatu; iṉi
ā ē ō ammūṉṟum viṉā	(tol-eḻut-nūṉ: 32)
eṉavum,
māṟuko ḷeccamum viṉāvu miyamum
	(tol-eḻut-uyir: 88)
eṉavuṅ kūṟuvaṉavum avi. eṉṉi? iṭiccollōt tiṉuḷ viṉāveṉṟōtāta ākāram vārātatuṭaṉ āṇṭu viṉāveṉṟu ōtiya ēkāra ōkāraṅkaḷ
ā ē ō ammūṉṟum viṉā
eṉṟu uṭaṉ kūṟiṉamiyāṉum, avvāṟu iṭiccol lōttiṉuḷ eṭuttōtāta māṟukoḷeccattoṭum aiyat tōṭum āṇṭōtiya viṉāviṉiyum eṇṇiṉiyum eḻuttōttiṉuḷ vitantuṭaṉ kūṟiṉamiyāṉumeṉpatu; itu nōkkip pōlum.
īṟṟaci yivvin tēkāram	(tol-col-iṭi: 9)
eṉa iṭiccolliṉi eḻuttuccāriyi peytōtiya karut tāṉē ipporuṇmikoḷḷa vippāṉāyiṟṟu meṉpatu.
maṟṟu ivi etiratupōṟṟalākāvōveṉiṉ,--- atu poruṭpaṭik kaṇṇateṉavum ivi āṭciyuṅ kuṟiyīṭum paṟṟiyatōr pakuti yeṉavum kūṟi viṭukka. aḵtēṟ, kuṟi yīṭṭāl īṇṭārāyāṉōveṉiṉ,--- ivi uttivaki yākalāṉum atu tāṉē uttiyeṉap paṭumātalāṉum ataṉi īṇṭu ārāyāṉeṉpatu.
(7)	vantatukoṇṭu vārātatuṇarttal --- piṉṉoru vaḻi vantatukoṇṭu muṉvārātatōr poruḷ aṟiya vittal; atu,
ñāṅkark kiḷanta valleḻuttu mikum
	(tol.eḻut-uyir: 2)
eṉpatu.
		eñciya mūṉṟu mēlvantu muṭikku
meñcuporuṭ kiḷavi yilaveṉa moḻipa
	(tol.eḻut-ecca: 43)
eṉa, vantatu koṇṭu mēṟkūṟappaṭṭa ēḻeccattiṟkum mēl vantu muṭikkuñ col vārātataṉi varumeṉṟuṇarntu koḷḷavittamiyiṉ ituvum atuvēyāyiṟṟu.
āyiru tiṇiyi ṉicikkumaṉa collē
	(tol-col-kiḷa : 1)
eṉṟavaḻit tiṇiyeṉṉum peyar epporuṭkum eytuvittal oru cūttirattuḷḷē kōṭaliṉ ataṉi ataṟku iṉameṉṟu koḷḷappaṭum; piṟavum aṉṉa.
(8)	muntu moḻintataṉ ṟalitaṭumāṟṟu---muṉṉoru kāṟ kūṟiya muṟiyaṉṟip piṉṉorukāl tali taṭumāṟākak kūṟutal; atu,
paṉṉī ruyirumoḻimuta lākum
	(tol-eḻut-moḻi : 26)
eṉavum,
uyirmey yallaṉa moḻimuta lākā
	(tol-eḻut-moḻi : 27)
eṉavum, uyirum meyyum niṟuttamuṟiyāṉaṉṟik,
		katana pamaveṉu māvin teḻuttu
mellā vuyiroṭuñ cellumār
	(tol-eḻut-moḻi : 28)
eṉavum meypaṟṟi variyaṟi kūṟutalum,
		ellā moḻikku miṟutiyu mutalu
meyyē yuyireṉ ṟāyī riyala	(tol-eḻut-puṇa : 1)
eṉa muṟkūṟiyamuṟi piṟaḻak kūṟutalumāyiṉa.
meyyum uyirum paṟṟi vitantu variyaṟuppiṉum aḵtu iraṇṭaṟkuñ cellumeṉṟu kōṭaṟkum, iṉi iyalpuvaki yāṉ īṟāka orutaliyāka uṭiyaṉa meyyeṉṟaṟkum avvāṟu kūṟiṉāṉeṉṉāmōveṉiṉ, aṅṅaṉamē ak karuttiṉāṉaṉṟē ottakāṭciyeṉak kuṟṟattōṭu oppumi kūṟi paṟṟup poruḷ payattaliṉ amiyumeṉṟu koḷvāmā yiṟṟeṉpatu.
iṉi,
mūṉṟu taliyiṭṭa muppatiṟ ṟeḻuttiṉ
	(tol-eḻut - puṇa : 1)
eṉṟāṟpōlvaṉa iṉa meṉappaṭum.
puḷḷi yillā vellā meyyum
	(tol-eḻut-nūṉ : 17)
eṉpatu mey muṟkūṟiṉamiyiṉ ituvum itaṟku utāraṇa meṉappaṭum.
maṟṟu, mayakkaṅkūṟalōṭu itaṉiṭi vēṟṟumiyeṉṉi yeṉiṉ, --- aḵtu
iṉṉē vaṟṟē	(tol-eḻut-puṇa : 17)
eṉa niṟuttamuṟiyāṟ kūṟātu maṟṟatuvē paṟṟāka maṟṟoru poruḷ koḷḷappaṭum. iḵtu iṉṉataṉṟi iṅṅaṉam mayaṅkak kūṟalvēṇṭum. poruṇmittāki varumeṉpatu; aḵtēl, itu māṟukoḷakkūṟaleṉṉuṅ kuṟṟamākāvō veṉiṉ, --- ākātu; eṉṉi? itu muṟkūṟiya poruḷi māṟupaṭāmi yāṉum niṟuttamuṟi talitaṭumāṟa vikkun tuṇiyākalāṉu meṉpatu.
(9)	oppakkūṟal : oṉṟu kūṟuṅkāl iruporuṭ kuṟitta teṉṟu iraṭṭuṟacceytal. atu,
		iṉṉi ṉikara māvi ṉiṟuti
muṉṉark keṭuta lurittu mākum
	(tol-eḻut-puṇa : 18)

	(tol-eḻut-uyir : 2)
eṉavum,
aṉṉa veṉṉu muvamak kiḷaviyum
	(tol-eḻut-uyar : 8)
eṉavum iṉamallaṉavaṟṟi uṭaṉeṇṇutalum,
māmarak kiḷaviyu māvu māvum
	(tol-eḻut-uyir, 29)
eṉa māṭṭeṟiyuṅkāl vēṟuvēṟu vitiyuṭiyaṉavaṟṟi oruṅku māṭṭeṟitalum, āṇṭu āṟu valleḻuttiṉiyum uṭaṉ kōṭalum, nilimoḻittoḻiloṭu varumoḻit toḻilum oppukkoṇṭu māṭṭeṟitalum pōlvaṉa oppakkūṟaleṉṉum pakutiyāy aṭaṅkumeṉpatu.
(10)	orutali moḻital---ōr atikārattiṟ collaṟ pālataṉi vēṟu atikārattuc colli avvilakkaṇamē āṇṭuṅ koḷḷavittal; atu,
a i u ammūṉṟum cuṭṭu	(tol-eḻut-nūṉ : 31)
eṉavum,
āṇum peṇṇu maḵṟiṇi yiyaṟki
	(tol-eḻut-puḷḷi : 8)
eṉavum ivi eḻuttatikārattuk kūṟiyavāṟṟāṉē collati kārattuḷḷum avvilakkaṇaṅ koḷḷa vittamiyiṉ appeyarttāyiṟṟu.
uṭampoṭupuṇarttuc colluvaṉa ataṟku iṉa meṉappaṭum. eṉṉi? vitiyallātatu vitipōla maṟṟoru vaḻic cēṟaliṉ.
(11)	taṉkōṭkūṟal---collātaṉa piṟavuḷavāyiṉum annūṟku vēṇṭuvatē koḷvaleṉṟal; atu,

	(tol-eḻut-piṟap : 20)
āyiṉum atu kūṟiṉēṉeṉavum, iyaṟcolliṟkallatu nili moḻiyākkaṅ kūṟēṉeṉavum,
vaḻakkuñ ceyyuḷum ārāyval
eṉṟu pukuntāṉ pāṭaluṭ payiṉṟa vaḻakkē kūṟuvaleṉavuṅ kūṟutaliṉ avi taṉkōṭkūṟutalām.
iṉi, oṉpatu mayakkattuṇ meymmayakkaṅkūṟi oḻintaṉa kūṟāmiyum viṉittokiyum paṇputtokiyum eṭuttōti muṭiyāmiyum,
		nāṭaka vaḻakkiṉu mulakiyal vaḻakkiṉum
pāṭal cāṉṟa pulaṉeṟi vaḻakkam
	(tol-akat : 53)
paṟṟip poruḷilakkaṇaṅ kūṟuvaleṉṟalum, perumpāṉmi ilakkaṇa vaḻakkeṉpa eṉṟalum, ataṉāṉē ciṟupāṉmiyi mayakkameṉṟalum, am ām em ēm eṉpaṉa mutalāyavaṟṟi aṅṅaṉam pakuttōtutaṟpayaṉum, avi viṉiyiṉṟi av viṉi ceytāṉmēl nikaḻkiṉṟa kūṟātalumē paṟṟi,
viṉiceyal maruṅkiṟ kālamoṭu varunavum
	(tol-col-iṭi : 2)
eṉṟu iṭiccolloṭu ōtutalum pōlvaṉa ataṟku iṉameṉappaṭum; eṉṉi? ivitāṅ kūṟuvaleṉṟu pukunta vaṟṟuḷḷum oru poruḷāṉavaṟṟi varintu koṇṭamiyiṉ avaṟṟuḷ viriyeṉappaṭṭaṉa.
(12)	muṟipiṟaḻāmi --- kāraṇamiṉṟit tāṉ cila poruḷ eṇṇi niṟuttiyapiṉṉar ammuṟi piṟaḻntāluṅ kuṟṟamil vaḻiyum am muṟiyiṉiyē ilakkaṇamākac collutal atu.

(13)	piṟaṉuṭampaṭṭatu tāṉuṭampaṭutal---uḷ poruḷ aṉṟāyiṉum vaḻakkiyalāṟ koḷporuḷ ituveṉak kūṟutal; atu,
paṇṭiyaṉ maruṅkiṉmarī iyamarapu
	(tol-col-vēṟ.ma: 7)
eṉṟāṟ pōlvaṉavaṟṟāṉ aṟika.
iṉi,
mīyeṉa marīiya viṭamvari kiḷaviyum
	(tol-eḻut-uyir : 48)
eṉpuḻi mēleṉpatu ilakkaṇameṉṟu eṭuttōtiyataṉi marūuveṉṟamiyiṉ atuvum ataṉpāṟpaṭum. eṉṉi? mutaṉūluṭkoṇṭavāṟaṟintu maṟṟu ataṉittāṉ iccol iṉṉavāṟāyiṟṟeṉṟu ilakkaṇaṅkūṟātu uṭampaṭutaliṉ. maṟṟu ataṉi iṉameṉpateṟṟukku? itutāṉē piṟaṉuṭam paṭṭatu tāṉuṭampaṭṭatākātō mutaṉūlāciriyaṉ uṭampaṭṭatākaliṉeṉiṉ---aṟṟaṉṟu; mutaṉūlāciriyaṉip piṟaṉeṉṉāmiyāṉum, mutaṉūliṉ vaḻittākiya nūluḷ avaṉ uṭampaṭṭa toṉṟu uṭampaṭumeṉṟu uttivakiyāṟ koḷḷātu muḻuvatūuṅ koḷḷumākalāṉum avvāypāṭu kūṟalākāteṉpatu.
iṉi, mutaṉūluḷ mēleṉpatu mīyeṉa marīiyiṟṟeṉṟu vitantōtappaṭṭataṉi aṅṅaṉaṅ kūṟātu mīyeṉa marīiyiṟ ṟeṉṟu vāḷātu kūṟiṉamiyiṉ, ataṉi iṉameṉṟu koṇ ṭāmeṉpatu; eṉavē, īṇṭup piṟaṉeṉṟatu vaḻakkiṉuḷ ḷōri nōkkiyāyiṟṟu; eṉṟāṟku oḻinta vaḻinūlāciri yarip piṟaṉeṉṟāṉeṉṉāmōveṉiṉ, avaruṭampaṭṭatu uṭampaṭṭataṉāṟ payantateṉṉi? mutaṉūliṟ piṟaḻāmi nūlceyyumāyiṉeṉa maṟukka.
allatūum icinūluṅ kūttanūlumpaṟṟip piṟaṉ kōṭ kūṟaleṉpataṉāṟ piṟaṉeṉṉiṉaṉṟi iyaṟṟamiḻk kaṇṇē mutaṉūlāciriyaṉit piṟaṉeṉṉāṉeṉpatu ; aḵtēl vaḻakkunūl, ceyvāṉ avvaḻakkiṉi vaḻaṅku vārip piṟaṉeṉṇumōveṉiṉ, ilakkaṇamum vaḻakkumeṉa iraṇṭaṉuḷ iḵtilakkaṇamātaliṉ avvaḻakkiṉuḷ vaḻaṅku vārip piṟaṉeṉṟāl amiyumaṉṟōveṉpatu ; eṉṟāṟku avaruṭampaṭṭatu uṭampaṭutal uttivakiyeṉpa teṟṟukku? aḵtu iyalpēyaṉṟōveṉiṉ,- aṅṅaṉam --- marīiyiṉum ilakkaṇameṉpatu tiripillātākaliṟ ṟiripupaṭum vaḻakkiṉi uṭampaṭutal ilakkaṇa mēyāmeṉpatu karuttu ; allākkāl eḷḷēpōla eṭkup piyun taṉ taṉmiyāṉ uḷ poruḷākalum vēṇṭu maṉṟē veṉpatu.

eṉat karuviyōttiṉuṭ cāriyi makaram pakaravarumoḻik kaṇ tiriyāteṉṟu pōyc ceykiyuḷ valveḻuttiṉatu iyaṟki melleḻuttātal aṟivittaṟku alvaḻi yellā melleḻuttāku meṉac collutal pōlvaṉa. ṅañana ātaṟkum iḵtokkum.
		ōmpaṭi yāṇiyiṟ kiḷantavaṟ ṟiyalāṟ
pāṅkuṟa vuṇarta leṉmaṉār pulavar
	(tol-col-uric 98)
eṉṟāṟ pōlvaṉa ataṟku iṉameṉappaṭum.
maṟṟut taṉkōṭkūṟalōṭu itaṉiṭi vēṟṟumi yeṉṉi yeṉiṉ, atu tantirañceyyum pakutikkaṇṇatu; iḵtu aṉṉataṉṟip puṇarccikkaṭ ciṟappuṭiya nilimoḻi varumoḻikkut tiripupōlāteṉṟu karuviyākiya iṭic coṟkāyiṉ ittuṇi yamiyumeṉṟu āṇi ceytaliṉ appeyarttāyiṟṟu.
(22)	palporuṭ kēṟpiṉ nallatu kōṭal --- oru cūttirattuṭ payanta coṟṟoṭar palaporuṭ kēṟṟatāyiṉum nallatu koḷkiṉṟāreṉak karuti avvāṟu ceytal: avi,
		oruva reṉṉum peyarnilik kiḷavi

	(tol-col-peya :37)

	(tol-col-peya :38)

numme ṉiṟuti yiyaṟki yākum
	(tol-eḻut- uru :15)

peyarviṉikku ōtiya ilakkaṇam oḻinta coṟkuñ cevvāṉañ cellumeṉṟu kōṭal ataṟku iṉameṉap paṭum piṟavum aṉṉa.
maṟṟu nutaliyataṟitaloṭu itaṉiṭi vēṟṟumi yeṉṉi yeṉiṉ, ---atu vāḷātu payamila kūṟiyatupōlak kūṟiyavaḻi ivvāṟu kūṟiyatu iṉṉa karuttup pōlu meṉṟu aṟiyavittalum, urivakiyāṉum nutaliyataṟiyac collutalumām; iḵtu aṉṉataṉṟi accūttiran taṉṉāṉ oruporuḷ payantataṉṟaliyum piṉṉoruporuḷ peṟa varutaliṉ ituvēṟeṉpatu.
maṟṟu ñāpakaṅ kūṟaloṭu itaṉiṭi vēṟṟumi yeṉṉi yeṉiṉ,---payamillatu pōlavum aritum peritumākavum iyaṟṟi eḷituñ ciṟitumāka iyaṟṟātu cūttirañ ceytal vēṟupāṭē nimittamākat tōṉṟik koḷvatoru poruḷ peṟa vittaliṉ ituvum vēṟeṉappaṭumeṉpatu.
meyppaṭa nāṭic colliya allapiṟa avaṇ variṉum --- uyttukkoṇṭuṇartaloṭu mēṟkūṟiya muppat tiraṇṭum iccūttirattuḷ eṭuttōtiya poruḷvakiyāṉ ārāyntu collappaṭṭaṉavaṉṟē? aṅṅaṉañ collātaṉa piṟavum innūluḷ variṉum; colliyavakiyāṟ curuṅka nāṭi---uttivakiyeṉa vakuttukkoṇṭu ōtiya muppattiraṇṭu pakutiyāṉ aṭaṅkumāṟu ārāyntu; maṉattiṉ eṇṇi mācaṟat terintukoṇṭu---ōtappaṭṭa utti palavum oruṅkuvariṉum uḷḷattāl teḷḷitiṉ ārāyntu mayakkantīra vēṟuvēṟu terintu vāṅkikkoṇṭu; iṉattil cērtti uṇarttal vēṇṭum---muppattiraṇṭākum ēṟṟavakiyāṉ iṉañcārtti maṟṟavaṟṟi iṉṉatituveṉap peyar kūṟal vēṇṭum; aṅṅaṉan tokaniṉṟa vaḻiyum vēṟuvēṟu koṇṭu; nuṉittaku pulavar kūṟiya nūlē---talimi cāṉṟa āciriyarāṟ kūṟappaṭṭa nūl (e---ṟu),
eṇṇiya muppattiraṇṭumallaṉa tōṉṟiṉum avaṟṟuḷ aṭakki, avitām oruṅkuvariṉum vēṟu terintu iṉan tōṟuñ cērttutali avāvi niṟkum īṇṭu ōtiya nūleṉpatu karuttu.
colliya alla piṟa avaṇ varumāṟum, avi colliyavakiyāṟ curuṅka nāṭi iṉattiṟ cērttumāṟum mēṟ kāṭṭappaṭṭaṉa.
iṉi, oruṅku palavutti vantavaḻi uḷḷattāl teḷḷiti ṉeṇṇitterintu koṇṭu iṉattiṟ cērttutal varumāṟu:

paṉmuṟi yāṉum parantaṉa varūum
uricco lellām poruṭkuṟi kūṭṭal

tōmpaṭi yāṇiyiṟ kiḷantavaṟ ṟiyalāṟ
pāṅkuṟa vuṇarta leṉmaṉār pulavar
	(tol-col-uri : 98)

		aṉṉa piṟavuṅ kiḷanta valla

ōmpaṭi yāṇi
eṉpatu āṇi kūṟalām. iṅṅaṉam oru cūttirattuṭ pala vantavaḻi oṉṟē uttiyeṉṟuṇarātu maṉattiṉeṇṇi mācaṟat terintukoṇṭu ivvāṟṟāṉ iṉattiṟ cērttuka eṉṟāṉ āciriyaṉeṉpatu.
		marapunili tiriyā māṭciya vāki
yuripaṭu nūṟā miruvaki niliya	(tol-mara. 93)
eṉpatu mutalāka ittuṇiyum vaḻakku nūlilakkaṇaṅ kūṟiṉāṉ. eḻunilatteḻunta ceyyuḷiṉ (476) ituvum oṉṟākaliṉ itaṉic ceyyuḷiyaliṟ kūṟiṉāṉ, īṇṭu marapu kūṟum vaḻi oḻinta ceyyuṭkup pōlātu, nūliṟkup poruṭpaṭi (yākiya yāppuk kuṟṟaṅkaḷum uttivakiyuṅ kūṟal vēṇṭutaliṟ kūṟiṉāṉeṉpatu aṅṅaṉaṅ kūṟākkāṟ pāṭṭiṉ marapu kaṭṭuri pōlavuṅ
kaṭṭaḷi araṅkiṉṟi vaṭṭā ṭiyatu*
pōlavum varampiṉṟi vēṇṭiyavāṟu nūl ceytal vilakkiṉṟā vāṉ cellum; cellavē, eḻucīrāṉākiya muṭukiyalaṭiyāṉum ettuṇiyum nīṇṭatoru vañcippāṭṭāṉum piṟavum vēṇṭiyavāṟuñ cūttirañ ceytalum, oruvaṉ peyariṉi avvac cūttirac ceyyuḷuṭ cārttuvakiyāṟ peytu kūṟalum, uṇarttappaṭum poruḷiṉi mutaṉūluṭ kiṭantavāṟu pōlātu marapu nili tiriyac ceytalum, nālvaki yāppoṭu māṟupaṭac ceytalum, vēṇṭiyavar vēṇṭiyavāṟṟāṟ cila ceytalum, vēṇṭiyavar vēṇṭiyavāṟṟāṟ cila poruḷkaḷi vēṟu tōṟṟikkoṇṭu nūl ceytalum vilakkiṉṟākal paṭumeṉpatu.
iṉi uttivakiyum avvāṟē iṉṟiyamiyātaṉaveṉap paṭum. eṉṉi? uṇarttappaṭum poruḷ ituveṉṟu aṟivittalum, eḻuttuc coṟporuḷeṉap pakuttuk koṇṭu atikārañ ceytalum, uṇarvu pulaṅkoḷḷumāṟṟāl tokuttuk kāṭṭalum, maṟṟu avaṟṟi vakuttuk kāṭṭiya vaḻip payamil kūṟaleṉṟu karutāmal atu taṉṉāṉoru payampaṭac ceytalum, mutaṉūlāyiṉa vellām nūṟ poruḷuṇartaṟkuk karuviyā kaluñ cūttiraccurukkattuk kētuvākalum uṭimiyiṉ aviyum vēṇṭappaṭṭaṉa veṉpatu.
iṉi, avaṟṟi ittuṇiyeṉa variyaṟākkāl et tuṇiyum palavāki ikantōṭutalum, vaḻinūṉ mutaṉūl vaḻittaṉṟākalum paṭum. muppattu mūṉṟeḻut teṉṟāṉāyiṉum appeṟṟittaṉṟi aṟupattāṟākak koḷḷavittāṉeṉṟu uttikūṟutalum uṭampaṭuvāṉātal cellumeṉa maṟukka.
iṉi,
nuṉittaku pulavar kūṟiya nūl
eṉṟataṉāṉē; pāyiracceyyuḷuñ cūttiracceyyuḷum āciriyappāvum veṇpāvum peṟṟu varutalum, av virupāvum peṟṟu varutalum, pāṭṭuppōla ellāvuṟuppum peṟutaṟkuc cellā veṉpatūum, māttiri mutalākap pāvīṟāka vanta patiṉoṉṟum vaṇṇaṅkaḷuḷ ēṟpaṉa koḷḷiṉuṅ koḷḷumeṉavum yāppuṟuppuk koḷḷuṅkāl īṇṭotiya marapuṅkoḷḷappaṭumeṉavuṅ koḷka. urikkuṅ kāṇṭikikkum ivaṟṟuḷḷum ēṟpaṉa aṟintu koḷka.
iṉṉum nuṉittaku pulavar eṉṟataṉāṉē * tantiramuñ cūttiramum viruttiyumeṉa mūṉṟum oruvarēyaṉṟi oṉṟu oruvar ceytalum, iraṇṭu ceytalum peṟappaṭumeṉṟalum, orucāli māṇākkarun tammiṭi nūl kēṭṭa māṇākkarum pāyirañ ceyyappeṟupa veṉṟalum, potuppāyiramuñ ciṟappup pāyiramumeṉa appāyirantām iraṇṭāmeṉṟalum, īvōṉṟaṉmiyum ītaliyaṟkiyuṅ koḷvōṉṟaṉ miyuṅ kōṭaṉmarapumeṉṟa nāṉkuṟuppuṭiyatu potup pāyirameṉṟalum, ataṉvaḻiyē kūṟappaṭuñ ciṟappuppāyiran tāṉ eṭṭilakkaṇa muṭitteṉṟalum, avi ākkiyōṉ peyarum vaḻiyum elliyum nūṟpeyarum yāppum nutaliya poruḷuṅ kēṭpōrum payaṉu (naṉṉūl : 47) meṉappaṭu meṉṟalum, avi tām nūṟkiṉṟiyamiyāveṉak koḷḷap paṭutaluṅ kūṟi muṭikka.
ciṟappuppāyirattāṉē nūlilakkaṇam oru vakiyāṉ uṇarappaṭum; potuppāyirattāṉē āciriyarum māṇākkarum nūlurittalum nūlkēṭṭalum mācaṟavaṟintu urinaṭāttu vārāka ataṉāṉē ivi nūṉmukattiṉiṉṟu nilāvu meṉpatu.
		olkāp perumpukaḻt tolkāp piyaṉaṭi
palkāṟ paravutu meḻuttoṭu
colkā maruporuṭ ṭokitikaḻ poruṭṭē
āyvuri :
ilakkaṇa nūlāciriyaṉ tāṉkūṟa  eṭuttukkoṇṭa poruḷic cūttirattāṟ pulappaṭutta mēṟkoṇṭuuṇarttum muṟimiyē utti eṉappaṭum, itu tantiravutti eṉa aṭi puṇarttu vaḻaṅkappeṟum. tantiram---nūl. utti---yutti. tantira meṉiṉum nūleṉiṉum okkum. uttiyeṉpatu vaṭamoḻic citivu. atu cūttirattiṟkiṭappatōr poruḷ vēṟupāṭu kāṭṭuvatu eṉa viḷakkuvar iḷampūraṇar. uttiyiṉi nūṟ puṇarppu eṉa vaḻaṅkutalum uṇṭu. utti iṉṉa teṉpataṉi viḷakkum muṟiyil amintatu.
		nūṟporuḷ vaḻakkoṭu vāyppakkāṭṭi
ēṟpuḻi yaṟintitaṟ civvaki yāmeṉat
tarumvaki celuttutal tantiravutti
	(naṉṉūl---pāyiram cūt---15)
eṉavarum nūṟpāvākum.
ellā nūṟkum āvatōr ilakkaṇam uṇarttuvatāka amintatu.
		otta cūttira murippiṟ kāṇṭiki
meyppaṭak kiḷanta vakiya tāki
īriṅ kuṟṟamu miṉṟi nēritiṉ
muppat tiruvaki yuttiyoṭu puṇariṉ
nūleṉa moḻipa nuṇaṅku moḻip pulavar	(108)
eṉavarum tolkāppiya marapiyaṟ cūttiramākum. cūttirattiṉ poruḷi viritturikkumiṭattuk kāṇṭikiyuriyum, avvuriyālum viḷaṅkāk kālattu ataṉiyum viḷaṅka viritturikkum urivakiyum uṭiyatākip pattuvakik kuṟṟamum iṉṟi nuṇporuḷavākiyamuppattiruvakiyutti yoṭu porunti varuvatu nūlākum eṉṟu nuṇṇaṟivuṭiya pulavarkaḷ nūlukku ilakkaṇaṅ kūṟuvar eṉpatu ic cūttirattiṉ poruḷākum. itaṉkaṇ muppattiruvakiyutti eṉattokutturikkappaṭṭa uttikaḷi virittuk kūṟit tolkāppiyattiṉ mūṉṟatikāraṅ kaṭkum vēṇṭum puṟaṉaṭiyuṅ kūṟuvatu.
		otta kāṭci yuttivaki yurippiṉ
nutaliya taṟital atikāra muṟiyē
tokuttuk kūṟal vakuttumeyn niṟuttal
moḻinta poruḷō ṭoṉṟa vittal
moḻiyā tataṉi muṭṭiṉṟi muṭittal
vārā tataṉāl vantatu muṭittal
vantatu koṇṭu vārātatu muṭittal
muntu moḻintataṉ talitaṭu māṟṟē
oppak kūṟal orutali moḻiyē
taṉkōṭ kūṟal uṭampōṭu puṇarttal
piṟaṉuṭam paṭṭatu tāṉuṭam paṭutal
iṟantatu kāttal etiratu pōṟṟal
moḻivā meṉṟal kūṟiṟ ṟeṉṟal
tāṉkuṟi yiṭutal orutali yaṉmi
muṭintatu kāṭṭal āṇi kūṟal
palporuṭ kēṟpiṉ nallatu kōṭal
tokutta moḻiyāṉ vakuttaṉar kōṭal
maṟutali citittut taṉṟuṇi purittal
piṟaṉkōṭ kūṟal aṟiyā tuṭampaṭal
poruḷiṭiyiṭutal etirporu ḷuṇarttal
colli ṉeccam colliyāṅ kuṇarttal
tantupuṇarn turittal ñāpakaṅ kūṟal
uyttukkoṇ ṭuṇarttaloṭu meyppaṭa nāṭi
maṉatti ṉeṇṇi mācaṟat terintukoṇ
ṭiṉattiṟ cērtti yuṇarttal vēṇṭum
nuṉittaku pulavar kūṟiya nūlē	(tol-marapu-112)
eṉavaruvatu tolkāppiya marapiyaliṉ iṟutic cūttiramākum. itaṟku iḷampūraṇarum pērāciriyarum cila viṭaṅkaḷil vev vēṟu pāṭaṅkoṇṭu uri varintuḷḷaṉar.
iccūttirattil otta kāṭci utti eṉa aṭi koṭut tōtiṉamiyāl muṉṉic cūttirattiṟ kūṟappaṭṭa pattuvakik kuṟṟattōṭum ottu nōkkum niliyatu utti eṉavum utti eṉṟamiyātu uttivaki eṉat terittuṇarttiṉa miyāl nūlāciriyaṉ nuṇṇuṇarviṉ tiṟattāl iyalpāka amiyum nūṟpuṇarppu muṟiyē utti eṉappaṭum eṉavum, in nūṟpāvil nutaliyataṟital mutalāka eṭutturikkappaṭuvaṉa nūlāciriyaṉ taṉ karuttuppaṭi ceyaṟkiyāka amittuk koḷḷum muṟimiyātaliṉ uttivaki eṉappaṭṭa eṉavum, uttivakivirippiṉ eṉavē iṅkuk kūṟappaṭum uttivaki ovvoṉṟiṉum iṉamāka aṭakkappaṭuvaṉa uḷa eṉavum koḷvar pērāciriyar,
pattu vakik kuṟṟattōṭum ottuvarum eṉavē ivi nūṟkaḷ aṉṟi oḻinta ceyyuṭku varuṅkāl vilakkappaṭutalum, muṟkūṟiya kuṟṟampōla iviyum vēṟucila poruḷ paṭittaluṭiyavāyiṉa. kāṭci yutti eṉṟu ivaṟṟik kūṟiyavataṉāṉ nūluṭ kāṇappaṭum. aintu kuṟṟattōṭum (kūṟiyatu kūṟal, māṟukoḷak kūṟal, mikippaṭak kūṟal. (poruḷil moḻital, mayaṅkak kūṟal eṉṉum aintum maṟṟoruporuḷ koḷḷiṉ avi vaciyaṟṟaṉa vātal pōla avvannūṟkup payaṉpaṭavarum pakutiyāṉ) ottal koḷḷappaṭum eṉpar pērāciriyar. uktiyeṉpatu ceyaṟki vakiyākiya nūṟ puṇarppāyiṉ nūl ceyyuṅkāl iyalpu vakiyākiya vaḻakkuñ ceyyuḷum pōlac collutal oṇmiyuṭiyataṉṟu eṉiṉ, avvāṟu cevvaṉam (iyalpu vakiyāṟ) collutal nuṇmiyoṭu puṇarnta oṇmit tātal vēṇṭum eṉpatu (āciriyaṉ) muṉṉarc colliṉāṉām. eṉavē aṅṅaṉam oṇmiyuṭiyatātalē utti eṉpatu tāṉē viḷaṅkum. cevvaṉañ collāta tantiravutti vakiyum avvāṟē oṇmiyuṭiyavām eṉpatu iccūttirattiṉ karuttākum. eṉavē cevvaṉañ ceytalē utti eṉavum nutaliyataṟital mutalāka iṅkuc collappaṭṭavi utti vakiyeṉavum koḷvar pērāciriyar.
muṉṉar (marapiyal-98) etiratu nōkki muppattiru vakiyutti eṉat toki kūṟip piṉṉar (iccūttirattil)
		maṉatti ṉeṇṇi mācaṟat terintu koṇ
ṭiṉattiṟ cērttiyuṇarttal vēṇṭum
eṉkiṉṟārākalāṉ aṅṅaṉam iṉampaṟṟi avaṟṟōṭu aṭaṅkuvaṉavellām avaṟṟuviriyākum eṉṉuṅ karuttiṉāl utti vaki virippiṉ eṉṟār āciriyar.
avaṉiva ṉuvaṉeṉa varūum peyarum (col-164) eṉpataṉuḷ, tokiyiṉṟiyuñ curuṅkac ceyvatōr āṟu (upāyam) uḷatāyiṉum avi patiṉintu peyarumē oru nikaraṉaveṉpatu aṟiyalākum vaṇṇam appatiṉintum eṉat toki kūṟutal veḷḷitaṉṟi oḷḷitākavē yamitaliṉ tokuttuk kūṟal uttivakiyāṉ amiyum eṉavum, iṉi meypeṟu marapiṟṟoṭivaki tāmē (cey-101) eṉṉuñ cūttiram pōlvaṉa piṟitoru poruḷ payakkumeṉṟalum accūttirattuṭ kāṭṭappaṭṭatu eṉavum ivvāṟṟāl ivvutti vaki ellā nūṟkum iṉṟiyamiyāvāyiṉa eṉavum kūṟuvar pērāciriyar.
iyaṟkip poruḷi yiṟṟeṉak kiḷattal (col-19) eṉṟāṟ pōlac collic celvaṉa cevvaṉañ ceyyum uttiyākaliṉ avi uttivakiyeṉappaṭā uttiyeṉa vēpaṭum eṉpatu pērāciriyar karuttākum. iccūttirattiṟ kuṟikkappaṭṭa muppattiraṇṭu uttivaki iviyeṉavum avvakikku iṉamāka virittuk koḷḷappaṭuvaṉa ivi yeṉavum pērāciriyar utāraṇaṅ kāṭṭi viḷakkiyuḷḷār.
eṇṇiya muppatiraṇṭumallaṉa tōṟṟiṉum avaṟṟuḷ aṭakki avitām oruṅkuvariṉum vēṟu terintu nūl colliya alla piṟavarumāṟum avi colliya vakiyāṟ curuṅkanāṭi iṉattiṟ cērttumāṟum pērāciriyaruriyiṟ kāṭṭappaṭṭaṉa.
ivvuttivaki paṟṟi iḷampūraṇarum pērāciriyarum koṇṭa pāṭaṅkaḷ uttivakiyiṉ peyarkaḷiṟ cila māṟṟattik kuṟippaṉavākum.
moḻinta poruḷōṭoṉṟa vittal, moḻiyāta taṉi muṭṭiṉṟi muṭittal eṉa iḷampūraṇar koṇṭa iraṇṭuttikaḷiyum moḻinta poruḷoṭoṉṟa av vayiṉ moḻiyātataṉi muṭṭiṉṟu muṭittal eṉa orē uttiyākak koṇṭār pērāciriyar.
vantatu koṇṭu vārātatu muṭittal eṉa iḷam pūraṇarum vārātatuṇarttal eṉap pērāciriyarum pāṭaṅ koṇṭaṉar.
taṉkōṭ kūṟal uṭampoṭu puṇarttal
eṉa iḷampūraṇarum
taṉkōṭkūṟal muṟipiḻimi eṉap pērāciriyarum
pāṭaṅ koṇṭaṉar.
orutaliyaṉmi muṭintatu kāṭṭal eṉpataṉi iḷam pūraṇar orē uttiyākak koṇṭār, pērāciriyar orutali yaṉmi eṉavum muṭintatukāṭṭal eṉavum iraṇṭākak koṇṭār,
piṟaṉuṭampaṭṭatu tāṉuṭampaṭutal eṉpataṟkup piṟaṉūṉ muṭintatu tāṉuṭampaṭutal eṉavum mūla pāṭam kāṇappaṭutalāl, piṟanūṉ muṭintatu tāṉuṭampaṭutal eṉa yāpparuṅkala viruttiyiṉum naṉṉūl mayili nātaruriyilum kāṇappaṭum uttiyum ituvum oṉṟēyeṉpatu naṉku teḷiyap paṭum.
moḻiyātataṉi muṭṭiṉṟi muṭittalāvatu, eṭut tōtāta poruḷi muṭṭuppaṭāmal uriyiṉāṉ muṭittal. itaṉi uriyiṟkōṭal eṉpa eṉpar iḷampūraṇar.
vārātataṉāṉ vantatu muṭittal vantatu koṇṭu vārātatu muṭittal eṉṉum uttikaḷ collamippil naṉṉūlil iṭam peṟavilli. naṉṉūlil varum oṉṟiṉa muṭittal taṉṉiṉa muṭittal eṉpatu ōrutti itaṉi iraṇṭuttikaḷākak koṇṭu uyttuṇaravippu eṉpataṉi uttikaṭkellām aṭimoḻiyākkutalum uṇṭu. vārātatu koṇṭu vantatu muṭittali oṉṟiṉa muṭittal eṉavum, vantatu koṇṭu vārātatu muṭittalit taṉṉiṉa muṭittal eṉavum koḷḷutaṟkum iṭamuṇṭu.
muntumoḻintataṉ tali taṭumāṟṟam eṉpataṉit talitaṭumāṟṟan tantu puṇarnturittal eṉpatōr utti eṉpar kāriki yuriyāciriyar.
oppakkūṟal eṉpatu oppiṉ muṭittal māṭṭeṟin toḻukal eṉa naṉṉūlil varum uttikaḷōṭu collālum poruḷālum ottuḷa oṉṟu kūṟuṅkāl itu poruṭ kuṟitta teṉṟu iraṭṭuṟac ceytal, eṉap pērāciriyar viḷakkutalāl iḵtu iraṭṭuṟa moḻital eṉṉum utti eṉpatu avar karutteṉat terikiṟatu.
orutalimoḻiyāvatu ēkākkarameṉṉum vaṭamoḻip poruṇmi. aḵtāvatu cūttirattukkup poruḷ kavarttut tōṉṟiṉ ataṉuḷ oṉṟiṉit tuṇantu kūṟal eṉpar. iḷam pūraṇar. oru tali moḻital eṉpataṉi naṉṉūlil varum orutali tuṇital eṉṉum uttiyākak koḷḷalām. taṉ kōṭ kūṟal uṭampoṭupuṇarttal eṉpatu iḷampūraṇar koṇṭa pāṭam. naṉṉūlilum ivvāṟē kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. taṉkōṭ kūṟal muṟipiṟaḻāmi eṉap pāṭaṅ koḷvar pērāciriyar.
14,  15.	iṟantatu kāttal etiratu pōṟṟal eṉṉum iraṇṭum yāpparuṅkalaviruttiyilum naṉṉūlilum iṟantatu vilakkal  etiratu pōṟṟal eṉa āḷappeṟṟaṉa. kāttal eṉṟatu kuṟṟamē kākka (tirukkuṟaḷ-434) eṉpuḻippōla vilakkutal eṉṟa poruḷil āḷappeṟṟatu.
16.	moḻivām eṉṟal eṉpatu ivvirunūlkaḷilum urittum eṉṟal eṉak kuṟikkap peṟṟatu.
17.	kūṟiṟṟeṉṟal eṉpatu, naṉṉūlil urittām eṉṟal eṉak kuṟikkappeṟṟatu.
18.	tāṉ kuṟiyiṭutal eṉpatu yāpparuṅkalaviruttiyilum naṉṉūlilum taṉ kuṟi vaḻakkam mikaveṭutturittal eṉak kāṇappaṭukiṟatu.
19.	orutaliyaṉmi muṭintatu kāṭṭal eṉpataṉi ōruttiyākak koṇṭu oruporuḷiyōtiyavaḻic collu taṟkē yurittaṉṟip piṟa poruṭkum potuvāka muṭittami kāṭṭal eṉa viḷakkam tantār iḷampūraṇar. 19,20.  oru taliyaṉmi eṉpatu iraṇṭu moḻital eṉavum muṭintatu kāṭṭal eṉpatu muṭiviṭaṅkūṟal eṉavum yāpparuṅkala viruttiyilum naṉṉūlilum kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷa uttikaḷākak koḷḷalāmā eṉpatu āyvukkuriyatākum.
21.	āṇi kūṟal eṉpatu yāpparuṅkala viruttiyilum naṉṉūlilum kāṇappaṭvilli.
22.	palporuṭkēṟpiṉ nallatu kōṭal itaṉi ēṟpuḻikkōṭal eṉavum orupuṭic cēṟal eṉavum collupa eṉpar pērāciriyar.
eṭutta moḻiyiṉ eytavittal
eṉṟunaṉṉūlil varum utti iṅkuk karutattakkatu.
23.	tokutta moḻiyāṉ vakuttaṉar kōṭal
colliṉ muṭiviṉ apporuḷ muṭittal
	(naṉṉūl)
eṉpatum atu eṉpar iḷampūraṇar.
24.	maṟutali citittut taṉṟuṇipurittal eṉpatu yāpparuṅkalaviruttiyilum naṉṉūlilum iṭampeṟavilli.
25, 26.	piṟaṉkōṭ kūṟal eṉpataṉiyum aṟiyātuṭam paṭal eṉpataṉiyum yāpparuṅkalaviruttiyum naṉṉūluriyum piṟaṉūṉ muṭintatu tāṉuṭampaṭutal eṉṟa collamippilēyē aṭakkiyiruttal kūṭum.
27.	poruḷiṭiyiṭutal eṉpatu oru poruḷi yōtiya vaḻi, ataṟkiṉamākiya poruḷic cērak kūṟātu iṭiyīṭupaṭak kūṟutal eṉpar iḷampūraṇar. naṉṉūliluḷḷa muṉmoḻintu kōṭal piṉṉatu niṟuttal eṉṉum iraṇṭu uttikaḷum itaṉkaṇ aṭaṅkum.
28.	etirporuḷuṇarttal eṉpatu naṉṉūlil varum etiratu pōṟṟal eṉṟa uttiyilō allatu muṭiviṭaṅ kūṟal eṉṟa uttiyilō aṭaṅkum.
29.	colliṉeccam colliyāṅkuṇarttal eṉpatu yāpparuṅkalaviruttiyilum naṉṉūlilum varum eñciya colliṉ eytak kūṟal eṉṉum uttiyātal kūṭum.
30.	tantupuṇarn turittal eṉpatu, naṉṉūlil varum ētuviṉ muṭittal eṉṉum uttiyātal kūṭum.
31.	ñāpakaṅkūṟalāvatu, iraṇṭuṟamoḻintu iraṇṭu coṟkaḷum poruḷkōṭal eṉpatu iḷampūraṇar. cūttiram ceyyuṅkāl aritum peritumākac ceytu maṟṟum ataṉālē vēṟu pala poruḷuṇarttal eṉpar pērāciriyar.
32.	uyttuk koṇṭuṇartal --- iḵtu uytturi vippu eṉa yāpparuṅkala viruttiyilum naṉṉūlilum iṭam peṟṟuḷḷatu.

uyttuṇara vippeṉa yuttiyeṇ ṇāṉkē.
(i-ḷ) ivi muppattiraṇṭum tantiravuttiyāvaṉa, ivaṟṟuṭ cilavaṟṟi māṟṟic colluvārum muppattiraṇṭiṉ mēlum pala veṉpārumuḷar. aviyum aṟintu koḷka eṉpar naṉṉūluriyāciriyar mayilinātar.
tolkāppiya marapiyal kūṟum muppattiraṇṭuttikkuḷ nutaliyataṟitali nutalippukutal eṉavum, atikāra muṟimiyiṉi ōttumuṟi vippu eṉavum, tokuttuk kūṟalit tokuttuc cuṭṭal eṉavum, vakuttu meynniṟuttali vakuttuk kāṭṭal eṉavum, oppakkūṟali iraṭṭuṟa moḻital eṉavum oppiṉ muṭittal eṉavum orutali moḻitali orutali tuṇital eṉavum, piṟaṉuṭam paṭṭatu tāṉuṭam paṭutalip piṟanūṉ muṭintatu tāṉuṭaṉpaṭutal eṉavum, iṟantatu kāttali iṟantatu vilakkal eṉavum etiratu pōṟṟali etiratu pōṟṟal eṉavum moḻivāmeṉṟali urittumeṉṟal eṉavum, kūṟiṟṟeṉṟali urittāmeṉṟal eṉavum, tāṉ kuṟiyiṭutalit taṉkuṟivaḻakka mika veṭut turittal eṉavum muṭintatu kāṭṭali, muṭiviṭaṅkūṟal eṉavum piṟaṉkōṭ kūṟalip piṟaṉ kōṭ kūṟal eṉavum, colliṉeccam colliyāṅkuṇarttal eṉpataṉi eñciya colliṉ eytakkūṟal eṉavum koḷḷalām.
palporuṭkēṟpiṉ nallatu kōṭali ēṟpuḻikkōṭal eṉpar uriyāciriyarkaḷ. moḻinta poruḷōṭoṉṟa avvayiṉ moḻiyātataṉi muṭṭiṉṟi muṭittal eṉpataṉi uriyiṟ kōṭal eṉpar iḷampūraṇar.
yāpparuṅkala viruttiyāciriyar ivi pāṭalaṉār uriyeṉak kuṟitta 32 tantiruttikaḷuḷ muṭittukkāṭṭal, tāṉeṭuttu moḻital, coṟporuḷ virittal, ētuviṉ muṭittal eṭutta moḻiyiṉ eytavittal, iṉṉatalla tituveṉa moḻital, oṉṟiṉa muṭittal, taṉṉiṉa muṭittal, pāṭṭeṟin toḻukal, muṉmēṟ kōṭal, piṉṉatu niṟuttal, eṭuttuk kāṭṭal, muṭintatu muṭittal, colliṉ muṭiviṉ apporuḷ muṭittal, toṭarc coṟ puṇarttal, yāppuṟuttamittal, vikaṟpattumuṭittal, tokuttuṭaṉ muṭittal eṉum 17 uttikaḷ tolkāppiyattil iṭam peṟavilli. ivaṟṟuḷ yāppuṟut tamittal tokuttuṭaṉ muṭittal eṉum iraṇṭum nīṅkalāka ēṉiya patiṉintum naṉṉūlil uḷḷaṉa.
tolkāppiyaṉār, pāṭalaṉār naṉṉūlār kūṟiya uttivakiyuḷaṭaṅkāta vēṟu cila uttikaḷum paḻiyavurikaḷil iṭam peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. yāpparuṅkalak kārikiyuriyāciriyar tali taṭumāṟṟan tantu puṇarnturittal eṉṉum ōr uttiyit tammuriyiṟ kuṟittuḷḷār. tolkāppiya vuriyāciriyarkaḷum naṉṉūluriyāciriyarkaḷum tattam urikaḷiṟ kuṟippiṭum uttiyiṉ peyarkaḷum avaṟṟukkuriya viḷakkaṅkaḷum eṭuttuk kāṭṭukkaḷum oppunōkki ārāyntu variyaṟukkat takkaṉavākum.
tolkāppiyattiṉ iṟutippakutiyākiya marapiyal piṟ kālattiṟ pala māṟṟaṅkaḷip peṟṟuḷḷateṉpatu,
		vaṭuvil kāppiya maturavāyp poruḷmarapu vīṭṭiyatāl
vaḻutiyāṭciyi vaḷavaṉ māṟṟiṭa maturi kūppiṭunāḷ
eṉavarum oṭṭakkūttar vāymoḻiyāl uyttuṇarappaṭum immarapiyaliṟ kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷa muppattiraṇṭuttikaḷum kuṭalīya arttacāttirattiluḷḷa muppattiraṇṭu utti kaḷōṭu ottuk kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa eṉpar makāvittuvāṉ rā. irākaviyaṅkār avarkaḷ. (tamiḻ varalāṟu --- pakkam 318). tolkāppiya marapiyaliṟ kāṇappaṭum muppattiraṇṭutti kaḷukkum kuṭalīyattiluḷḷa muppattiraṇṭuttikaḷukkum colvakiyālum karuttu vakiyālum vēṟupāṭukaḷ uḷḷaṉa. iṟantatu kāttal, etiratu pōṟṟal,moḻivāmeṉṟal, aṟiyātuṭaṉ paṭal eṉat tolkāppiyattiluḷḷa uttikaḷukku otta vaṭamoḻip peyarkaḷit tēṭikkāṇa muṭivilli. muntu moḻintataṉ talitaṭumāṟṟam eṉṟataṉi apavarkkam eṉṟum, kūṟiṟṟeṉṟalip piratēcam eṉṟum, palporuṭ kēṟpiṉ nallatu kōṭali vikaṟpam eṉṟum, piṟaṉ kōṭ kūṟalip pūruvapakkam eṉṟum, etirporuṇart tali viyaryayam eṉṟum, ñāpakaṅ kūṟali apatēcam eṉṟum, avarkaḷ kuṟippiṭṭiruppatu avvuttikaḷiṉ poruḷiyipukku muṟṟilum māṟupaṭukiṟatu. eṉavē ivvuttikaḷiṟ perumpālāṉa ottirup paṉavāka makāvittuvāṉ irākaviyaṅkāravarkaḷ kūṟiyiruppatu attuṇip poruttamuṭiyatākat tōṉṟa villi. tolkāppiya marapiyalilum kuṭalīya artta cāttirattilum iṭam peṟṟuḷḷa uttikaḷ muppatti raṇṭutti eṉṉum tokiyaḷavil ottaṉavāyiṉum avaṟṟiṉ ilakkaṇa amippil vēṟupaṭṭaṉavākavēyuḷḷaṉa. iv vēṟupāṭṭiṉiyuḷaṅ koṇṭa makāvittuvāṉ aiyaṅ kāravarkaḷ ivvuttikaḷ vaṭamoḻi nūlip piṉpaṟṟiyē  amintiruttal vēṇṭum eṉṟum tamatu koḷkiyi vaṟpuṟuttum karuttiṉāy ivvuttivakiyil tolkāp piyarkkum kuṭalīyarkkum ottamutaṉūl iḵteṉṟu iṉṉun tuṇitaṟkilliyeṉṟum aintiram niṟinta tol kāppiyaṉ eṉappāyiraṅ kūṟutalāl avvintirattēṉum ataṉ vaḻittākiya piṟitoru nūlilēṉum ivvuttikaḷ uḷḷaṉa veṉṟu niṉikkappaṭum eṉṟum aiyuṟukiṉṟār. utti eṉṉum col vaṭa collāyiṉum tolkāppiya marapiyalil uttiyeṉṉuñ collāṟ kuṟikkappaṭum nūṟpuṇarppākiya muṟimit tamiḻilakkaṇa nūlkaḷukkuriya ciṟappuṭiyatātaliṉ itaṟku mutaṉūlāka vaṭanūl oṉṟu iruttal vēṇṭum eṉa ūkippatum avvūkattiṉiyaṭippaṭiyākakkoṇṭu tol kāppiyar kālatti uṟutippaṭutta eṇṇuvatum porutta muṭiyaṉa alla. artta cāttiram iyaṟṟiya kuṭalīyar tamiḻ nāṭṭiṟ kāñcinakarattu vāḻntavarātaliṉ, avar kālattukku muṟpaṭṭa tolkāppiya marapiyaliluḷḷa uttikaḷ muppattiraṇṭiṉi yuḷaṅkoṇṭu tāmiyaṟṟum poruḷ nūlamippukku ēṟṟa vakiyil tām ceyta arttacāttirattilum muppattiraṇṭu uttikaḷi vakuttuk kūṟiyuḷḷār eṉak koḷvatē tolkāppiyar kuṭalīyar ākiya ivviruvar varalāṟṟukkum kāla amitikkum ēṟpuṭiyatākum eṉat teḷital eḷitākum.

tolkāppiyam, poruḷatikāram, marapiyal
urivaḷam muṟṟiṟṟu.

*	kuḻaviyō ṭiḷimip peyarē eṉpatu pērāciriyar pāṭam, pērāciriyar ellāviṭattum iḷimi eṉṟē kuṟippiṭuvār.
1.	poruṭku marapāvaṉa poruḷiṉ kuṇamākiya iḷami, āṇmi, peṇmi ākiya poruṭpeṟṟi kuṟittut toṉṟutoṭṭu vaḻaṅki varum marapup peyarkaḷ.
2.	irutiṇip poruḷum paṟṟi varum iḷamippeyar uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu. u.vē.
3.	pārppu mutalākak kuḻaviyīṟākac collappaṭṭa oṉpatum iḷamip peyar eṉṟavāṟu.
	kuḻaviyoṭu oṉpatum eṉa moḻimāṟṟuka.
 1.	aviyāmāṟu tattañ cūttirattuk kāṭṭutum. u.vē. ittolkāppiyac cūttiratti,
		pārppum paṟaḻum kuṭṭiyum kuruḷiyum
		kaṉṟum piḷḷiyum makavum maṟiyum
		kuḻaviyum eṉṟivi iḷamippeyarē	(i. vi. 910)
	eṉa eṭuttāḷavar vittiyanāta nāvalar.
2.	poruḷkaḷiṉ iḷamipaṟṟiya marapup peyarkaḷi eṭuttōtiya tol kāppiyaṉār avaṟṟiṉ mutumi paṟṟiya marapup peyarkaḷi eṭuttōtātatu avaṟṟukku variyaṟiyiṉ mi paṟṟiyēyām.
1.	māṟṟaruñ ciṟappiṉ marapiyal eṉṟataṉāl, ivviyaliṟ kūṟappaṭum marapup peyarkaḷ ekkālattum tattam marapiṟ piṟaḻāmal ceyyuḷ ceyyappaṭum eṉpatum, ivviyaliṟ kūṟappaṭāta marapukaḷāyiṉ avi ceyyuḷiṉpam vēṇṭi vaḻakkoṭu poruntiya marapiṟ piṟaḻavum ceyyuḷ ceyyappeṟum. eṉavē, avi māṟṟutaṟku uriyavātalum kūṭum eṉpatum kūṟiyavāṟu,
*	iruṭuṇittaṉṉa
1.	ēṟum ēṟṟiyum eṉpatē tolkāppiyaṉār kūṟiya mūlapāṭam. innuṭpam ivviyal 40, 42-ām nūṟpākkaḷāl naṉku tuṇiyappaṭum. erutum ēṟṟiyum eṉa iḷampūraṇar urip patippiluḷḷa pāṭam piṟkālattavarāl tirikkappaṭṭatākum.
2.	ēṟu mutalākac collappaṭṭaṉavum attaṉmiya piṟavum āṇpāṟ peyar eṉṟavāṟu. u.vē.
3.	āṇ, viṭi eṉpaṉa, vilaṅkukaḷuḷ āṇmi paṟṟi vaḻaṅkum marapup peyarkaḷākum.
	innūṟpā ilakkaṇa viḷakkattil 916-ām cūttiramāka eṭuttāḷap peṟṟuḷḷatu. appar eṉṟa col piṟkālattil vaḻakku vīḻntatu. atupaṟṟiyē mūṉṟāmaṭiyil utaḷum apparum eṉpataṉi utaḷum umpalum eṉa māṟṟiṉar ilakkaṇa viḷakka āciriyar.
1.	iṅku āṇmi paṟṟiya marapup peyarkaḷuḷ oṉṟāka eṭutturikkappaṭṭa pōttu eṉpatu cila viṭaṅkaḷil iḷami kuṟitta peyarākavum vaḻaṅkumāyiṉum āṇmikkuc ciṟantu varumāṟupōla iḷamikku attuṇic ciṟantu varutal illi.
2.	antañ cāṉṟaveṉpatu vaḻakkiṉkaṇ muṭiyavamittateṉṟavāṟu. u.vē.
1.	kaṭami eṉpatu māṉiṉattuḷ oru cātiyik kuṟitta peyarākavum vaḻaṅkutaluṇṭu. āyiṉum iccūttirattil eṭuttōtappaṭṭatu, peṇmi paṟṟiya marapup peyareṉak koḷka.
2.	antam -- muṭipu. antañcāṉṟa -- muṭittuk kūṟappaṭṭa eṉavē, ivvāṟu peṇmi paṟṟiya peyar eṉa uṟutiyākak kūṟappaṭāta ā eṉpatu orukāl peṇmip peyarāka vaḻaṅkappeṟutalum, āṇ, peṇ eṉṉum marapup peyarkaḷ puṟattē vayiramuṭiya pullukkuriyaṉavāka vaḻaṅkap peṟutalum koḷḷappaṭum. āṉēṟu eṉpatu āṉiṉattuḷ ēṟu eṉapporuḷ tarutaliṉ iṅku ā eṉpatu peṇmi paṟṟiya peyarākātu potuvākap pacu eṉṉum iṉam paṟṟiya peyarāka vaḻaṅkappaṭutaliṉ ippeyar antañcāṉṟa peṇpeyarākāmi naṉku teḷiyappaṭum.

pullukkum āṅkē pociyumām	(mūturi-10)

1.	pārppiyal, araciyal, vāṇikat toḻilumāki mūṉṟu varuṇattārkkum potuppaṭa niṉṟa ōtal, vēṭṭal, ītal eṉpaṉa immūvarkkum ōtta ciṟappiṉa eṉavum, vēḷāḷarkkum vāṇikarkkum uriya nirikāttalum vāṇikamum ottaṉa ciṟappiṉavākāmal orōvoṉṟu orō varuṇattārkku uriyaṉavām ātalāṉum, immarapiyalil avaṟṟi āciriyar vitantu kūṟiṉār eṉpatu mēṟkuṟitta viṉāvukkup pērāciriyar tarum viṭiyākum.
2.	nirikāvalum uḻavuttoḻilum vaṇikarkkum vēḷāḷarkkum uriyavāy varutal pōlātu, vāṇika vāḻkki vēḷāḷarkkum uḻutuṇṭal vaṇikarkkum ciṟupāṉmiyāy varum eṉavum eṇvakik kūlaṅkaḷi viṟṟalē vaṇikarkkup perumpāṉmiyāy varum eṉavum ic cūttiraṅkaḷāl āciriyar kūṟiṉār eṉpar pērāciriyar. nālvaki varuṇap pākupāṭṭiṉi aṭiyoṟṟiyuri yeḻutiya pērāciriyarum nacciṉārkkiṉiyarum tamiḻakac camutāya niliyi aṭiyoṟṟiyuri kūṟumiṭattu vaṇikariyum vēḷāṇ māntariyumotta iṉattavarākavē kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷami iṅkuk kūrntu nōkkat takuvatākum.
1.	vēntu viṭu toḻilāl paṭiyum kaṇṇiyum vēḷāṇ māntar peṟuvar eṉa vitikkum iccūttiram muṉṉuḷḷa 77-ām cūttirattōṭu muraṇpaṭutal kāṇka. ivviyal 84-ām nūṟpāvilēyē iccūttira viti aṭaṅkiyuḷḷami kāṇka.

cūttiraṅkaḷip paṟṟiya aṭikkuṟippu	
	naṉṉūliṟ potuppāyiramāka aminta cūttiraṅkaḷ pavaṇanti muṉivarāl iyaṟṟappaṭṭaṉa alla eṉpatu. naṉṉūluriyāciriyarkaḷuḷ kālattāl muṟpaṭṭa mayili nātar accūttiraṅkaḷip pavaṇanti muṉivar ceytaṉavākak koḷḷāmal malartaliyulakiṉ eṉat toṭaṅkum ciṟappup pāyiravuriyiṉmuṉ paḻañ cūttiraṅkaḷākat tantu, piṉṉarc ciṟappup pāyirattiṟku uri varintuḷḷār. iṅṅaṉamākavum piṉ vanta uriyāciriyarkaḷākiya caṅkara namaccivāyap pulavar mutaliyōr, ippotuppāyirac cūttiraṅkaḷum pavaṇanti muṉivarāl iyaṟṟappaṭṭaṉavē eṉak koṇṭu uri varintuḷḷaṉar. nūlāciriyar ellōrum ellā nūlukkumuriya potuppāyira ilak kaṇattit tam nūliṟ colla vēṇṭum eṉṉum iṉṟiyamiyāmiyilli. ākavē appakuti pavaṇanti muṉivar vākkeṉa uṟutiyākak koḷḷutaṟkilli. atupōlavē coṟporuḷ marapuṇarttum tolkāppiya marapiyaliṟ kāṇappaṭum ilakkaṇa nūl marapu paṟṟiya iccūttiraṅkaḷ paṇṭināḷil marapiyaluriyil eṭuttāḷappaṭṭa paḻañ cūttiraṅkaḷātal kūṭum eṉak karutavēṇṭiyuḷḷatu. tolkāppiyam ceyyuḷiyalil nūlippaṟṟiyum cūttirattik kuṟittum tolkāppiyaṉār kūṟiya moḻi naṭikkum avipaṟṟi marapiyaliṟ kāṇappaṭum moḻinaṭikkum vēṟupāṭu mikutiyum uṇmi iṅku niṉittaṟkuriyatākum.
1.	marapunili tirittuc colluvarāyiṉ ulakattuc collellām poruḷiḻantu vēṟupaṭum eṉpataṉi mēl ivviyal 91-ām cūttirattāl ilakkaṇa nūlukkum eytuvitta āciriyar, aṅṅaṉam marapunili tiriyāmiyē nūlkaṭkut takutiyāmeṉavum aṅṅaṉam iyaṟṟappaṭum nūl mutaṉūl, vaḻinūl eṉa iruvakippaṭumeṉavum iccūttirattāṟ kūṟiṉār. ic cūttira mutalāka ilakkaṇa nūliṉ marapu kūṟappaṭukiṉṟatu.
2.	oru nūli mutalākak koṇṭu oruvaṉ vaḻinūl ceyyiṉ avvaḻinūl paṟṟip piṉṉoru kālattu oru nūl tōṉṟiya tāyil aṅṅaṉam tōṉṟiya nūl vaḻinūlākavum ataṟku mutalākavuḷḷa vaḻinūlē mutal nūlākavum vaḻaṅkappeṟum eṉṟaṟku, nutaliya neṟiyiṉ uripaṭu nūltām mutalum vaḻiyum eṉa iruvaki iyala eṉṟār āciriyar.
1.	piṉ tōṉṟiya vaḻinūlē taṉ mutalākakkoṇṭu vaḻinūṟku muṉṉuḷḷa mutal nūli nōkkac cārpunūlumām ākaliṉ iṉṉatē mutalnūl, iṉṉatē vaḻinūl eṉṉum variyaṟiyiṉmiyiṉ cārpunūl eṉpatōr vakiyiṉi āciriyar kūṟārāyiṉār. cārpunūl eṉpataṉi nūliṉ vakiyākak koṇṭāl, ataṉvaḻit tōṉṟiya nūlic cārpiṟ cārpu eṉavum,  ataṉvaḻi vantataṉic cārpiṟ cārpiṟ cārpiṟ cārpu eṉavum ivvāṟu eṇṇiṟantu cellumātaliṉ aviyellām aṭaṅka mutalnūl vaḻi nūl eṉṉum iruvakiyiṉiyē āciriyar kuṟippiṭuvārāyiṉar.
2.	mutal nūliṟ kūṟappaṭṭaporuḷip piṉṉar ōr upakārap paṭōr āciriyaṉvaḻi nūl ceytakkāl ataṉiyaṭiyoṟṟic cārpunūl eṉa oruvaṉ nūl ceytataṉāṟ payaṉyātu? eṉa viṉaviṉārkku, vaḻinūlum piṟkālattu arumiyuṭiya tāyiṉ ataṉiyum eḷitākac ceytalē payaṉ eṉa viṭi kūṟuvar pērāciriyar.
1.	etirnūl eṉpatum oṉṟuṇṭu. atuyārō mutaṉūliṉ muṭinta poruḷi oruvaṉ yātāṉum oru kāraṇattāl piṟaḻavittāl, ataṉik karuviyāl tirivu kāṭṭi oruvāki vittataṟku oḷḷiyāṉ oru pulavaṉāl urikkappaṭuvatu; eṉṉi?

	kaṇṭatu--ceytatu. kāṇutal--ceytal

1.	ivvāciriyar ekkālattum kuṟṟattukku ilakkaṇaṅ kūṟa māṭṭār. ilakkaṇattōṭu iyittuk kūṟumaḷavukku ikkuṟṟaṅkaḷ payaṉpaṭutaliṉ īṇṭuk kūṟiṉār. ceyyuḷukku āyiṉ ikkuṟṟaṅkaḷ poruntāc ciṟappiṉa. iṅkuk kūṟa eṭuttukkoṇṭatu, vaḻakkum ceyyuḷumēyaṉṟi avaṟṟiṉ vēṟupaṭa varum nūliṉ ilakkaṇamātalāṉum tiṇi, pāl, marapu, viṉā, ceppu, iṭam, kālam eṉpaṉapaṟṟi varum eḻuvaki vaḻukkaḷum amittuk koḷḷappaṭumāṟu pōṉṟu ikkuṟṟaṅkaḷuṭ cila amittuk koḷḷappaṭum eṉavum, aṅṅaṉam amittuk kōṭaṟkuriya kuṟṟaṅkaḷi iṉañcārttiyum, oḻintaṉavaṟṟi vēṟupaṭa vittum variyaṟuttuk kūṟiṉār āciriyar. citivu-kuṟṟam. citiveṉappaṭuvaṉavaṟṟi vaciyaṟa nāṭiṉ eṉṟataṉāl, ivvāṟu kuṟṟam eṉṟu variyappaṭṭavaṟṟik karuviyākak koṇṭu pukuntu avaṟṟāṟ pulaṉākum maṟṟoru poruḷi ārāyvōmāṉāl, akkuṟṟaṅkaḷuḷ vaci yaṟṟaṉavāy amittukkoḷḷappaṭuvaṉavum uḷa. aṅṅaṉam poruḷ payappaṉavāka amittukkoḷḷappaṭuvaṉa kūṟiyatu kūṟal, māṟukoḷak kūṟal miki paṭakkūṟal, poruḷila moḻital, mayaṅkak kūṟal eṉṉum ivvintumām eṉavum kūṟi iccūttiravuriyil pērāciriyar utāraṇaṅkāṭṭi viḷakkiyuḷḷami kūrntuṇarattakuvatākum.
2.	āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉār yāṇṭum ilakkaṇamē kūṟi, ilakkaṇattiṟ piṟaḻtalik kuṟṟam eṉṟu koḷḷa vippatallatu nūlukku illāta kuṟṟaṅkaṭku ilakkaṇam kūṟum vaḻakkamuṭiyārallar. avviyalpiṉarāciriyar īriṅkuṟṟaṅkaḷi iccūttirattil eṭuttuk kūṟiyatu etirmaṟuttuṇariṉ tiṟattavum avi eṉa varukiṉṟa cūttirattāl iviyum upakārap paṭutal nōkki eṉpatu pērāciriyar tarum viḷakkamākum.
1.	mēl ilakkaṇavaḻakkiṉiyē vitantu kūṟi, ataṟku māṟupaṭṭatu kuṟṟameṉak koḷḷavitta āciriyar, ivviyalil īriṅkuṟṟam eṉac cilakuṟṟaṅ kūṟiṉār; iviyum ilakkaṇamē kūṟiṉaṉ eṉpār etirmaṟuttuṇariṉ tiṟattavum aviyē eṉa akkuṟṟaṅkaḷi etirmaṟuttuk koḷḷum muṟiyil nūṟkuriya kuṇaṅkaḷ pattiṉiyum kuṟippiṟ pulappaṭa vittār. ak kuṇaṅkaḷāvaṉa kūṟiyatu kūṟāmiyum, māṟukoḷak kūṟāmiyum, kuṉṟakkūṟāmiyum mutaliyaṉavām.
	immarapiyaṟ cūttirapporuḷi aṭiyoṟṟik kūṟum muṟiyil amintatu,
muṉpakkattoṭarcci
		curuṅkac collal, viḷaṅkavittal,
naviṉṟōrk kiṉimi, naṉmoḻi puṇarttal,
ōciyuṭimi, āḻamuṭit tātal,
muṟiyiṉ vippē ulakamali yāmi,
viḻumiyatu payattal, viḷaṅkutāraṇatta
tākutal nūliṟ kaḻakeṉumpattē	(naṉṉūl--13)
eṉavarum cūttiramākum.

tāmviḷi koḷḷā eṉmaṉār pulavar
